The core decrypts the whole body in memory and only then checks, in order: the authenticity of all chunks, the length `P = padme(size)`, that the remainder after `size` bytes is all zeros, `BLAKE3(content) = content_hash`. An error at any step — nothing is returned (С5).                                                                                                                                                                                             | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф23 | The AAD of every chunk is the whole preamble (32 bytes). This way tampering with the version, the epoch or the prefix breaks every chunk. The order of chunks and truncation are protected by the counter and the flag in the nonce.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф24 | Parsing: after the preamble, while more than 65,552 bytes remain, an ordinary chunk of 65,552 bytes is read; the remainder (16…65,552 bytes) is the last chunk. A remainder shorter than 16 bytes or extra bytes at the end is an error (the tag will not match). The first version's interface works with the whole body in memory; a streaming variant with the same format is added together with the engine (Э3). Requirement for Э3: streaming open writes to a temporary file next to the target and publishes it atomically only after verifying all chunks, the length, the zeros and the hash; memory is bounded meanwhile. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф25 | The epoch in the preamble is compared with the VK's epoch; any mismatch, including 0, is `EpochMismatch`. The order of checks before decryption: the magic string (`Malformed`), the version (`UnsupportedVersion`), the epoch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### А.5. Padmé

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Note                    |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Ф26 | For `L < 2` the result equals `L`. Otherwise: `E = ⌊log₂ L⌋`, `S = ⌊log₂ E⌋ + 1`, `z = E − S`, `m = 2^z − 1`, the result is `(L + m) & ~m` (algorithm 1 in the paper by Nikitin et al., PETS 2019). Examples: `9 → 10`, `17 → 18`, `257 → 272`, `1000 → 1024`, `65 537 → 67 584`. The overhead is at most 12%. The body size on the server is `32 + P + 16 · max(1, ⌈P / 65 536⌉)`. An `L` larger than `2^62` is a `SizeTooLarge` error, without a panic or overflow: `padme` and `sealed_body_len` return `Result`, `open_body` checks `size` as its first action. `2^62` itself is allowed. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### А.6. Recovery code

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Note                    |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Ф27 | The code is 32 random bytes (256 bits). The alphabet is Crockford base32: `0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ` (no `I`, `L`, `O`, `U`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф28 | Encoding: bits are read left to right in groups of 5 bits. 256 bits give 51 full groups and a 52nd of one bit and four zero bits (the value `0` or `16`). That makes 52 characters.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф29 | Checksum: the first 20 bits of `SHA-256("amby-sync/recovery-check/1" ‖ the 32 bytes of the code)`, written as four characters in the same alphabet. It catches a typo with probability `1 − 2⁻²⁰` (this is not protection against guessing: the space is 256 bits). That makes 56 characters in total.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф30 | Notation: 56 characters in uppercase, 14 groups of 4, separated by `-` (`XXXX-XXXX-…`). Parsing: spaces, line breaks and `-` are ignored, case does not matter, `O → 0`, `I` and `L → 1` (the Crockford rules); everything else, including `U`, is an error; the length after cleaning is exactly 56; the zero bits of the 52nd character and the checksum are verified, otherwise an error. The order of checks: characters, length, zero bits of the 52nd character, checksum. The keys are derived from the 32 bytes of the code directly: `HKDF(code, "amby-sync/recovery-auth/1")` and `HKDF(code, "amby-sync/recovery-kek/1")`. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### А.7. Randomness and the order of drawing on it

The core takes randomness only through the `Random` interface. So that the reference and Rust produce the same
bytes, the order of draws is fixed: the vectors record a "stream of random bytes", the Rust test feeds
it in order and checks that all of it was consumed.

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Note                    |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Ф31 | Account creation: salt (16), AK (32), the nonce of the password wrapper (24), the recovery code (32), the nonce of the code wrapper (24). A new password by code, or a password change: salt (16), nonce (24). A new VK: 32 bytes. An AK or VK wrapper: nonce (24). A header envelope: nonce (24). A body: nonce prefix (19). A new identifier: 16 bytes. A new code: 32 bytes. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| Ф32 | All other operations take no randomness: they are deterministic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### А.8. Opus decisions (20 October)

1. Ф6, NFKC for the password, accepted (NIST SP 800-63B); spaces are not trimmed.
2. Ф8 accepted: the lower limits equal the defaults, the reason is written in Ф8 itself.
3. Ф15 accepted. Ф16 changed: duplicate keys are `Malformed`.
4. Ф17 accepted.
5. Ф22 and Ф24 accepted; streaming open moved into a requirement for Э3 (a temporary file, atomic publication after all checks).
6. Ф29 accepted: protection against typos, not against guessing.
7. Ф26 refined: an `L` larger than `2^62` is a `SizeTooLarge` error.
8. Ф14 accepted; consistency of the fields (a folder has no body, a folder's `size` is zero, and so on) is a requirement for the Э3 tests.
9. The whole-body-in-memory interface is kept, see item 5.
10. Ф3 accepted; Ф18 and Ф25 refined: any epoch mismatch, including 0, is `EpochMismatch`.
11. New: Ф33, path syntax.

## Appendix Б. Protocol v1

Here the protocol between the device and the server is fixed before the code (section 6, Э2, step 0). The conformance suite
`sync/conformance`, the server and the Э3 client are written **from this appendix**. It refines the API table from Р11 and
decisions Р1, Р3, Р5, Р11 and section 5, but does not override them. Each decision is a separate row with a number `П…`.
All of them were verified by Opus on 20 October 2026 (the note "verified by Opus, 20.10"). If a decision changes,
the tests and the server change in one separate commit after review.

Notation. `hexN` is a string of `2N` characters (that is, `N` bytes) `0-9a-f` (lowercase, no `0x`). Times `*_ms` are
an integer number of Unix milliseconds, UTC; they are only for display and cleanup and do not affect versions (Р3, С10).

### Б.0. General rules

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | Note                    |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П1  | All paths start with `/v1`. Requests and responses with a body are JSON in UTF-8 (`Content-Type: application/json`), except record bodies: they go as `application/octet-stream` (П22, П24). A different content type on a JSON request is `400 bad_request`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П2  | **Binary data in JSON is lowercase hex**, as in Ф1 and Ф2. Hex was chosen over base64url because: (a) appendix А already writes identifiers and hashes that way, and there is one rule for the whole format; (b) there is no padding, no two alphabets and no arguments about case; (c) tests and logs are readable by eye. The cost: headers in JSON are 2 times longer than their bytes, not 1.33 times. A thousand headers of 600 bytes each come to about 1.2 MB instead of 0.8 MB; record bodies never go into JSON at all. Uppercase letters, an odd length, characters outside `0-9a-f` or a wrong field length are `400 bad_request`. The error does not name the value. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П3  | Identifiers (`space_id`, `item_id`, `blob_id`, `commit_id`, `device_id`) are `hex16`, `instance_id` is `hex16`. `blob_id` equals the `body_id` from the header (Ф2, Ф20): the device chooses it itself on every body upload. The server does not check that they match, it does not see the header.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П4  | **Errors.** Any response with a 4xx or 5xx code is JSON `{"error": {"code": "<machine code>", "message": "<text for people>", "details": {…}}}`. `details` is not always present. The client looks at the HTTP code and `code`, and never parses `message`. The list of codes is in table Б.0.1.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П5  | **Authorization.** The device token is 32 random bytes, that is `hex32` (64 characters). It is passed only in the header `Authorization: Bearer <token>` (not in the URL: URLs end up in proxy logs). The server stores the SHA-256 of the token. A missing header, an unknown token or one of the wrong form is `401 unauthorized`; the token of a revoked device is `401 token_revoked`. Without a token only `/v1/info`, `/v1/auth/prelogin`, `/v1/auth/enroll`, `/v1/auth/login`, `/v1/auth/recover` are available.                                                                                                                                                          | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П6  | **Ownership.** In the first version a space has one owner (Р14 will add members). Someone else's space, someone else's device, someone else's record and nonexistent objects all answer the same `404 not_found`: the server does not reveal that an object exists.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П7  | **Unknown fields** in a request's JSON are ignored by the server, in a response's JSON they are ignored by the client (Р13): new protocol fields do not break old participants. A required field of the right form but with a wrong value is `400 bad_request`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П8  | **Limits** are shown by `/v1/info` (П9) and enforced by the server: a JSON request body is no larger than `max_json_bytes` (`413 payload_too_large`); a batch is no larger than `max_commit_items` records (`400 too_many_items`); a header envelope is no larger than `max_header_bytes` (`400 bad_request`); a record body is no larger than `max_blob_bytes` by **size after encryption** (`413 blob_too_large`; the client compares `sealed_body_len(size)`, not the file size). The defaults are in Б.9.                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

#### Б.0.1. Machine error codes

| HTTP | `code`                  | When                                                                                               |
| ---- | ----------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 400  | `bad_request`           | not JSON, a missing field, a wrong form or length of a field, a wrong content type                 |
| 400  | `too_many_items`        | the commit has more than `max_commit_items` records                                                |
| 400  | `duplicate_item`        | the same record twice in one commit                                                                |
| 400  | `bad_part`              | the number or length of a body part is wrong (П22)                                                 |
| 401  | `unauthorized`          | no token, unknown token                                                                            |
| 401  | `token_revoked`         | the token of a revoked device                                                                      |
| 401  | `invalid_credentials`   | a wrong password or recovery code, an unknown name, an account without a password (П15)            |
| 401  | `invalid_enroll_code`   | the enrollment code is wrong, already used, or the name is unknown (П17)                           |
| 401  | `enroll_code_expired`   | a correct code, but more than 24 hours have passed (П17)                                           |
| 403  | `invalid_proof`         | a wrong proof on a key change (П18)                                                                |
| 404  | `not_found`             | no such object, someone else's object (П6), an unknown path                                        |
| 405  | `method_not_allowed`    | the path exists, the method is wrong                                                               |
| 409  | `version_conflict`      | the record's base version did not match, the batch is rejected as a whole (П27)                    |
| 409  | `commit_id_reused`      | the same `commit_id` with different content (П28)                                                  |
| 409  | `keys_conflict`         | `base_keys_version` is stale (П18)                                                                 |
| 409  | `space_exists`          | a space with that `space_id` already exists (П20)                                                  |
| 409  | `blob_exists`           | a body with that `blob_id` has already been started with a different size (П21)                    |
| 409  | `parts_missing`         | `complete` before all parts are uploaded (П23)                                                     |
| 413  | `payload_too_large`     | JSON larger than `max_json_bytes`                                                                  |
| 413  | `blob_too_large`        | the body is larger than `max_blob_bytes` (П8)                                                      |
| 416  | `range_not_satisfiable` | the range is outside the body (П24)                                                                |
| 422  | `unknown_blob`          | the commit refers to a body that does not exist, is not complete or belongs to another space (П30) |
| 429  | `rate_limited`          | too many failed sign-in attempts (П16); the `Retry-After` header is in seconds                     |
| 500  | `internal`              | a server error; details only in the log                                                            |
| 501  | `not_implemented`       | a stub: the handler is not written yet                                                             |
| 507  | `quota_exceeded`        | the sum of the user's encrypted bytes would exceed the quota (П42)                                 |

### Б.1. Server information and `instance_id`

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Note                    |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П9  | `GET /v1/info`, no token, `200`: `{"server": "amby-sync-server", "server_version": "0.1.0", "protocol": {"min": 1, "max": 1}, "formats": {"header": 1, "body": 1}, "instance_id": "<hex16>", "time_ms": N, "limits": {"max_blob_bytes", "part_size", "max_commit_items", "max_header_bytes", "max_json_bytes", "changes_page_default", "changes_page_max", "login_max_failures", "login_window_secs", "sse_keepalive_secs", "login_max_failures_per_user", "login_window_per_user_secs", "quota_bytes_per_user"}, "retention": {"version_days", "orphan_blob_hours", "enroll_code_hours"}}`. All numbers are integers, except `quota_bytes_per_user`: it is `null` while the quota is off (П42). `part_size` is always 8,388,608 (8 MiB, Р11). | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П10 | **`instance_id`** is 16 random bytes that the server creates when it creates its database and stores in it. Restarting the server on the same data does **not change** it; an empty data directory (a reset, a lost volume, С7) gives a **new** one. The device compares it with the remembered one (Р10) and stops when it changes (Р8). It also arrives in every feed response (П31). It is not a secret.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.2. Accounts, sign-in, keys, devices

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | Note                    |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П11 | **The username** is ASCII, 1–64 characters from `a-z 0-9 . _ -`, the first and last character a letter or a digit. ASCII case is folded before validation: `Alice`, `ALICE` and `alice` are one login `alice`. The canonical lowercase name is used for storage, lookup, the availability check and attempt limiting. For `prelogin`, `login` and `recover`, an invalid name behaves like an unknown one (never `400`, П13), so that from the response one cannot tell "no such name" from "wrong thing typed". This name is seen by the server (О2).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | owner's decision, 12.11 |
| П12 | **`POST /v1/auth/enroll`** (the first device, Р5). Request: `{"username", "enroll_code", "salt": hex16, "params": {"memory_kib", "iterations", "parallelism"}, "auth_key": hex32, "wrapped_ak": hex73, "recovery_auth_key": hex32, "wrapped_ak_recovery": hex73, "device": {"name_enc": hex}}`. The server checks the code (П17), the form of the fields and the `params` limits per Ф8 (weaker than the limit is `400 bad_request`: О8), stores the SHA-256 of both `auth_key`s (Ф5), the salt, the parameters, both wrappers, creates the device and the token and **burns the code** — all in one transaction. Response `201`: `{"device_id", "token", "keys_version": 1}`. Reusing the same code is `401 invalid_enroll_code` (П17). `name_enc` is the device name encrypted per Ф34: **exactly 105 bytes**, otherwise `400 bad_request` (the same in `login`, `recover` and `POST /v1/spaces`); the server stores it as is and does not decrypt it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П13 | **`POST /v1/auth/prelogin`**, `{"username"}` → `200 {"salt": hex16, "params": {…}}`. For **any** name string (up to 256 characters) the response is a `200` of the same form. For a real user with a password these are their salt and parameters. For everyone else — an unknown name, an invalid name, a name without a password (the enrollment code not yet used) — it is a **fake salt**: the first 16 bytes of `HMAC-SHA256(prelogin_secret, "amby-sync/prelogin/1" ‖ username)`, where `prelogin_secret` is 32 random bytes that the server creates once and stores in the database. So a repeat (including after a restart) gives the same salt; different names get different salts; the parameters are the defaults (Ф7). A non-string `username` is `400 bad_request`. Prelogin does not count as a sign-in attempt (П16).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П14 | **`POST /v1/auth/login`**, `{"username", "auth_key": hex32, "device": {"name_enc"}}` → `200 {"device_id", "token", "salt", "params", "wrapped_ak", "keys_version"}`. Every successful sign-in creates a **new** device and a new token. A wrong `auth_key`: `401 invalid_credentials`, **no token is issued** (С12), nothing is created.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П15 | **`POST /v1/auth/recover`**, `{"username", "recovery_auth_key": hex32, "device": {"name_enc"}}` → `200 {"device_id", "token", "wrapped_ak_recovery", "keys_version"}`. The same device token as at sign-in. A wrong key is `401 invalid_credentials`. **The response to wrong credentials is identical** for an unknown name, a name without a password, a wrong password and a wrong code: the same HTTP code, the same `code`, the same `message`, with no headers that depend on the case. Hash comparison is constant-time.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П16 | **Attempt limiting.** The following count as a failure: `invalid_credentials` on `login` and `recover`, `invalid_enroll_code` and `enroll_code_expired` on `enroll`, `invalid_proof` on `PUT /v1/keys`. **Two counters** (both count unknown names the same way, so that `429` cannot be used to tell a real name). **(a) Strict — per the pair "name + client address":** when `login_max_failures` failures (5 by default) have accumulated over the last `login_window_secs` seconds (900 by default), the next attempt from that address for that name gets `429 rate_limited` and a `Retry-After` header (seconds until release). **(b) Soft — per name for all addresses together:** `login_max_failures_per_user` failures (50 by default) within `login_window_per_user_secs` (3600 by default) lock the name for everyone. Thanks to (a), someone who knows the name cannot lock the owner out of signing in from their own address; (b) stops guessing from many addresses. **The client address** is the socket address. `X-Forwarded-For` (the last address of the list) is taken into account **only** with `AMBY_SYNC_TRUST_PROXY=1`; without it the header is ignored, all requests through a proxy have one address, and (a) degenerates into a strict per-name rule — this is safe. During a lock, `429` is returned **even for correct credentials**; a blocked attempt is not recorded. Failures older than the window are forgotten. A successful sign-in resets the pair's strict count; the soft count is reset only by time. The lock affects sign-in, not devices that already have a token: they keep working. Prelogin does not count as an attempt (П13). The count runs on the server's clock (the clock interface, Б.7). | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П17 | **The enrollment code** is single-use, 100 random bits in Crockford base32 (the Ф27 alphabet), 20 characters, shown in groups `XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX`. The server stores the SHA-256 of the normalized code (uppercase, without `-` and spaces) and the expiry. It is valid for **24 hours** from creation (`enroll_code_hours`, by the server's clock) and can be used once. A wrong one, a used one, or one for an unknown name is `401 invalid_enroll_code` (the same for all); a correct but expired one is `401 enroll_code_expired`. A repeated `user add` for a name without a password issues a new code and burns the previous one; for a name with a password it is an error (Б.8).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П18 | **`GET /v1/keys`** → `200 {"keys_version", "salt", "params", "wrapped_ak", "wrapped_ak_recovery"}`. **`PUT /v1/keys`**: `{"base_keys_version", "proof": {"kind": "password"\|"recovery", "auth_key": hex32}, "salt", "params", "auth_key", "wrapped_ak", "recovery_auth_key"?, "wrapped_ak_recovery"?}`. The last two fields go together or not at all: they replace the recovery code. The order of checks: the token; the form of the fields and the `params` limits (Ф8); the **proof** (`proof.auth_key` must match the hash of the current password or recovery code — this is the "old password" from Р5; a wrong one is `403 invalid_proof` and a failure for П16); the **version** (`base_keys_version` equals the current one, otherwise `409 keys_conflict` with `details: {"keys_version": N}`). Accepted — `200 {"keys_version": N+1}`, all in one transaction. The tokens of other devices remain valid (Р5).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П19 | **Devices.** `GET /v1/devices` → `200 {"devices": [{"device_id", "name_enc", "created_ms", "last_seen_ms", "current": bool}]}`, in creation order. `DELETE /v1/devices/{device_id}` → `204`: the device's token is revoked immediately, and any request with it is `401 token_revoked` (Р5). You can disconnect yourself. A revoked device disappears from the list. Someone else's or an unknown device is `404 not_found`. A repeated delete is `404`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.3. Spaces

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Note                    |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П20 | **`POST /v1/spaces`**: `{"space_id": hex16, "name_enc": hex, "key_epoch": 1, "wrapped_vk": hex73}` → `201 {"space_id", "seq": 0, "created_ms"}`. `space_id` is chosen by the device: it is part of the VK wrapper's AAD (Ф12), so the key cannot be wrapped in advance without it. `key_epoch` in the first version is only `1` (Ф3). An already taken `space_id` is `409 space_exists`. **`GET /v1/spaces`** → `200 {"spaces": [{"space_id", "name_enc", "key_epoch", "wrapped_vk", "seq", "created_ms"}]}`; `seq` is the number of the last accepted commit (0 for a new one). `name_enc` is the space name per Ф34, exactly 105 bytes, otherwise `400 bad_request`. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.4. Record bodies

The upload goes in parts so that a break does not lose what was done (С1). The paths below are relative to `/v1/spaces/{space_id}`.

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Note                    |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П21 | **`POST /blobs`**: `{"blob_id": hex16, "size": N}` (`N` is the size of the **finished encrypted** body in bytes, from 1; more than `max_blob_bytes` is `413 blob_too_large`). A new body: `201`. The same `blob_id` with the same `size`: **`200`** with the current state — this is how the device finds out what is already uploaded (resuming, С1). The same `blob_id` with a different size is `409 blob_exists`. Response: `{"blob_id", "size", "part_size": 8388608, "parts": max(1, ⌈size / part_size⌉), "uploaded": [numbers of the uploaded parts in ascending order], "complete": bool}`. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П22 | **`PUT /blobs/{blob_id}/parts/{n}`**, the body is raw bytes (`application/octet-stream`), `n` starts from zero. The length must be exactly `part_size` for all parts except the last, and for the last the remainder (`size − n · part_size`). Otherwise `400 bad_part`; a number `n ≥ parts` is also `400 bad_part`. A part can be **overwritten** until `complete` (a retry after a break): `200 {"part": n, "size": length}`. No body — `404 not_found`; the body already complete — `409 blob_exists`.                                                                                          | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П23 | **`POST /blobs/{blob_id}/complete`**: checks that all parts are present; concatenates them; `200 {"blob_id", "size", "complete": true}`. Parts missing — `409 parts_missing` with `details: {"missing": [n, …]}`, the body's state does not change. A repeat after success is `200` again. The server does not decrypt and does not verify authenticity (it is blind, О1); it checks only lengths.                                                                                                                                                                                                  | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П24 | **`GET /blobs/{blob_id}`**: `200` and the bytes of the whole body, `Content-Length`, `Accept-Ranges: bytes`. With the header `Range: bytes=a-b` (and `bytes=a-`) — `206`, `Content-Range: bytes a-b/size`, only the requested bytes. A range outside the body is `416 range_not_satisfiable`. An incomplete or nonexistent body is `404 not_found`. The body is available until it is removed by cleanup (Б.7): a reference from a commit is not required.                                                                                                                                          | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П25 | **A body without a commit** (С1). A body that no version refers to is deleted by the server after `orphan_blob_hours` (**24 hours** by default) from the **last action on it** (creation, uploading a part, `complete`), not from creation: a long upload of a large file must not vanish in the middle. This applies to incomplete bodies too. After deletion the body is `404 not_found` on any request; the same `blob_id` can be started over. A body that a version refers to is not touched by cleanup while the version lives (П35).                                                         | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.5. Commit

Path `POST /v1/spaces/{space_id}/commit`.

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Mark                    |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П26 | Request: `{"commit_id": hex16, "items": [{"item_id": hex16, "base_version": N, "header": hex, "blob_id": hex16 \| null, "deleted": bool}]}`. `base_version` 0 means "I am creating the record"; the new version is always `base_version + 1`, computed by the server (Р3). `header` is the Ф18 envelope, no shorter than 301 bytes (1 + 4 + 24 + 256 + 16) and no longer than `max_header_bytes`; the server does not parse it. `deleted` is an open flag for cleanup (Р6); it is not taken on the device's word. Response `200`: `{"commit_id", "seq": N, "committed_ms", "replayed": false, "items": [{"item_id", "version"}]}` in request order. From 1 to `max_commit_items` records; an empty list is `400 bad_request`; a repeated `item_id` within the batch is `400 duplicate_item`. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П27 | **Atomicity.** A batch is accepted only if **all** `base_version` values equal the current versions (for a nonexistent record the current version is 0). Otherwise it is rejected **as a whole**: `409 version_conflict`, `details: {"conflicts": [{"item_id", "base_version": as passed, "current_version": current}]}` — **all** conflicting records, not just the first. Nothing is applied: no versions, no `seq`, no body references. The check and the write are a single transaction, and commits of one space run strictly one after another (Р3): in a race exactly one of the commits with the same base is accepted. П30 violations are checked before versions.                                                                                                                  | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П28 | **`commit_id` and replays** (С2). An accepted commit is remembered together with its response. A replay with the same `commit_id` and the **same** batch content returns the earlier response unchanged (`200`, `"replayed": true`, the earlier `seq`), even if the records have changed since. The same `commit_id` with **different** content is `409 commit_id_reused`. A rejected commit (`409 version_conflict`) is not remembered: after fixing it, it can be sent with the same `commit_id`. Compared by the SHA-256 of the canonical encoding of `items`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П29 | **`seq`.** A number kept per space; for a new space it is 0; each accepted commit gets the next number in sequence, without gaps (rejected commits and replays do not use up a number). All records of one commit share the same `seq`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П30 | **Body references.** A record's `blob_id` must point to a **completed** body of **this** space, otherwise `422 unknown_blob` (and the batch is rejected as a whole). `null` is for folders and for deletion without a body. Several versions of a record may reference one body (a rename does not upload the body again). The server does not check that the body is not taken by another record: it does not see `body_id` in the header (open question Б.10-4).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.6. Change feed, versions, notifications

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Mark                    |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П31 | **`GET /v1/spaces/{space_id}/changes?since=C&limit=L`.** The feed is **compacted**, like `_changes` in CouchDB: each record appears in it only with its **latest version**, at the `seq` of that version. When a record is replaced by a new version, it leaves its old `seq` and appears at the new one; earlier versions are visible only in `versions` (П33). This way the Э3 engine works from state, not from a log: a new device does not download headers for 30 days and does not parse intermediate versions. `since` is the **cursor**: the `seq` after which changes are needed (0 by default — from the beginning). The cursor is simply the `seq` of the last received commit; the client stores it and does not make it up (Р10). `limit` defaults to `changes_page_default` (500) and is at most `changes_page_max` (1000): if larger, the maximum is used, not an error. `since` that is not a non-negative integer, or `limit` that is not an integer from 1, is `400 bad_request`. Response `200`: `{"instance_id", "seq": feed head, "since": C, "next": cursor for the next request, "more": bool, "gap": bool, "changes": [{"seq", "item_id", "version", "header": hex, "blob_id": hex16 \| null, "blob_size": N \| null, "deleted": bool, "device_id", "committed_ms"}]}`. Records go in ascending `seq`, and within a commit in request order. **A page does not split a commit:** it contains whole commits as they remain in the feed (that is, those records of the commit that have not been replaced later), until the sum of records exceeds `limit`, but at least one; so renaming a folder in one commit arrives whole (Р2). Records move in the feed **only forward**, so nothing is lost while paging: a record changed after a page was read will arrive on a later page. `next` is the `seq` of the last commit of the page (or `since` if the page is empty); `more` says whether there is more. `since` greater than the head is `200` with an empty list and `next = since`: a device that sees a `seq` lower than its own (С7) stops per Р8. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П32 | **Feed gap.** The server remembers `purged_seq` — the largest `seq` of the latest version among records **removed entirely** (П36). If `since < purged_seq`, the response has `"gap": true`: the device may have missed someone else's deletion and needs a full reconciliation (Р10). Otherwise `false`. Removed intermediate versions (П35) are not visible in the feed and do not cause a gap.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П33 | **`GET /v1/spaces/{space_id}/items/{item_id}/versions`** → `200 {"item_id", "versions": [{"item_id", "version", "seq", "header", "blob_id", "blob_size", "deleted", "device_id", "committed_ms", "superseded_ms": N \| null}]}`, from newest to oldest; includes **all live** versions (the latest and earlier ones, until cleanup removes them). No such record — `404 not_found`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П34 | **Notifications, `GET /v1/spaces/{space_id}/events`** with `Accept: text/event-stream` and `Authorization`. Response `200`, `Content-Type: text/event-stream`, a stream. Right after connecting — a `hello` event: `data: {"seq": head, "instance_id": "…"}`. Each accepted commit is a `commit` event with `id: <seq>` and `data: {"seq", "device_id", "items": record_count}`. Every `sse_keepalive_secs` (15) — a comment line `: ping`. Events contain neither headers nor paths: they only mean "check the feed". Missed events are not recovered: after a disconnect the device simply requests the feed from its cursor. Without a token — `401`. Another user's space — `404`. **The stream of a device whose token has been revoked (П19) is closed by the server no later than the next event or `: ping`**, that is, within `sse_keepalive_secs`; no events are sent to a revoked device.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.7. Version retention and cleanup

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Mark                    |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П35 | **Earlier versions — 30 days** (О4, Р8; `version_days`). Time is counted from the **replacement**: version N lives `version_days` days after version N+1 replaced it (`superseded_ms`), not after its creation. The latest version of a record is never removed while the record is alive. A removed version disappears from `versions`; it was not in the feed (П31), so the feed and `purged_seq` do not change. Its body is deleted if no live version references it any more.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П36 | **Deleted records — 30 days.** If the latest version of a record has `deleted: true`, then `version_days` days after its commit the server removes the **whole record** with all versions and bodies. Until then it is visible in the feed (as the latest version) and in `versions`, so a deleted item can be restored (О4). After cleanup `purged_seq` grows to the `seq` of its latest version (П32).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П37 | **Clock and cleanup.** The server takes time only from the `Clock` interface; `SystemClock` is the real clock, `ManualClock` is a clock for tests that can be moved. Cleanup (bodies without a commit, versions, records, enrollment codes, attempt counters) is a single `maintenance` operation: the server runs it every 10 minutes and before stopping; it can be invoked manually (`amby-sync-server maintenance`). Cleanup is **idempotent** and does nothing to what has not yet expired. It cannot be triggered from outside over HTTP. The check suite moves `ManualClock` and calls cleanup only on a server it started itself in-process; such checks run on this own server and are not run against a foreign address (a foreign clock must not be moved). | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.8. Administrator commands

The executable is `amby-sync-server`; the data directory and the rest come from the environment (Б.9). The commands work with the **same data** as the running server.

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Mark                    |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П38 | `serve` (the default) — start. `user add <name> [--json]` — creates a user and a one-time enrollment code (П17). Without `--json` the code is printed for a human with an explanation; with `--json` **one line** `{"username", "enroll_code", "expires_ms"}` goes to standard output, the rest to standard error. Exit code: 0 — success; 2 — invalid name or wrong parameters; 3 — the name already has a password (a repeated `user add` for a name **without** a password issues a new code and burns the earlier one, П17); 1 — other errors. `user list [--json]` — names, whether there is a password, the code's expiry. `user remove <name> --yes` — deletes the user, devices, spaces and bodies (irreversible; without `--yes` it refuses). After `user remove`, old tokens give `401 unauthorized` (they no longer exist), and the same name can be created again via `user add`: it is a new user without the earlier data. `maintenance` — one cleanup (П37). `backup <directory>` — an online copy of SQLite and bodies (Р11). | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П39 | `user add` issues the code **only** to standard output and **nowhere** writes it to the log; only the SHA-256 of the code remains in the database. In general the server does not write tokens, `auth_key`, codes, wrappers and request bodies to the log (Р11).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

### Б.9. Configuration and startup

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Mark                    |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| П40 | Environment variables only, all with the `AMBY_SYNC_` prefix. Unknown variables with this prefix are ignored; an invalid value of a known one is a startup refusal with a clear message. Default values: see the table below.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П41 | **HTTPS (Р11).** The server speaks HTTP behind a proxy (Caddy). `AMBY_SYNC_PUBLIC_URL` is the external address. It must start with `https://` unless the host is `localhost`, `127.0.0.1` or `[::1]` and `AMBY_SYNC_ALLOW_HTTP=1` ("on a local network") is not set; otherwise the server does not start.
