The client itself requires `https://` by the same rules.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 20.10 |
| П42 | **Quota** (Р11). If `AMBY_SYNC_QUOTA_BYTES_PER_USER` is set, `POST /blobs` gets `507 quota_exceeded` when the sum of the user's encrypted bytes with this body would exceed the quota (equality is allowed). The sum includes: bodies of all live versions (latest and earlier), unfinished uploads by the declared `size`, and deleted records until cleanup (П36); bodies of all of the user's spaces are counted together. The quota does not affect what is already stored: reading, restarting a known body (`200`), parts of an upload already started, `complete`, and commits of references to already uploaded bodies all go through. There is no quota by default. | verified by Opus, 20.10 |

| Variable                                | Default                 | Meaning                            |
| --------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ---------------------------------- |
| `AMBY_SYNC_DATA_DIR`                    | `/data`                 | SQLite database and `blobs/`       |
| `AMBY_SYNC_LISTEN`                      | `0.0.0.0:8080`          | address and port                   |
| `AMBY_SYNC_PUBLIC_URL`                  | `http://localhost:8080` | external address (П41)             |
| `AMBY_SYNC_ALLOW_HTTP`                  | `0`                     | allow `http://` for non-localhost  |
| `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_BLOB_BYTES`              | `536870912`             | largest body after encryption (П8) |
| `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_COMMIT_ITEMS`            | `10000`                 | records per commit                 |
| `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_HEADER_BYTES`            | `16384`                 | header envelope                    |
| `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_JSON_BYTES`              | `67108864`              | JSON request                       |
| `AMBY_SYNC_RETAIN_VERSION_DAYS`         | `30`                    | П35, П36                           |
| `AMBY_SYNC_ORPHAN_BLOB_HOURS`           | `24`                    | П25                                |
| `AMBY_SYNC_ENROLL_CODE_HOURS`           | `24`                    | П17                                |
| `AMBY_SYNC_LOGIN_MAX_FAILURES`          | `5`                     | П16                                |
| `AMBY_SYNC_LOGIN_WINDOW_SECS`           | `900`                   | П16                                |
| `AMBY_SYNC_SSE_KEEPALIVE_SECS`          | `15`                    | П34                                |
| `AMBY_SYNC_LOGIN_MAX_FAILURES_PER_USER` | `50`                    | П16, soft count by name            |
| `AMBY_SYNC_LOGIN_WINDOW_PER_USER_SECS`  | `3600`                  | П16                                |
| `AMBY_SYNC_TRUST_PROXY`                 | `0`                     | trust `X-Forwarded-For` (П16)      |
| `AMBY_SYNC_QUOTA_BYTES_PER_USER`        | off                     | П42                                |

### Б.10. Opus decisions on open questions (October 20)

1. **hex (П2)** accepted.
2. **Lockout by name (П16)** replaced by two counters: a strict one per "name + address" pair and a soft one per name (changed).
3. **Fake salt (П13)** accepted as a known limitation: the real salt changes on password change, the fake one does not; a real user's parameters may differ from the default values.
4. **Body and record are not linked on the server (П30)** — sufficient: the body key is derived from `body_id`, and the device catches a substitution (Ф20, Ф22).
5. **Feed gap (П32, П36)** accepted: a gap leads to a full reconciliation by hashes (Р10). `version_days` is already in `/v1/info`, a separate boundary is not needed.
6. **Device and space names (П12, П20)** are described in appendix А as Ф34; the check suite encrypts names exactly that way.
7. **The feed returns all versions (П31)** — withdrawn: the feed is compacted.
8. **Closing the stream on token revocation (П34)** — the server closes the stream (changed).
9. **Several key epochs (Р14)** accepted for v1: the list of epochs will appear later as a new field (П7).
10. **Quota (Р11)** is described as П42 (`507 quota_exceeded`).
11. **After `user remove`** old tokens give `401 unauthorized`, and the name can be created again (П38).
12. **No space `DELETE`** accepted for v1; "deleting a space from the server from within the app" has been added to the Э5 list.

The upper bound of a commit is 10,000 records (П8, Б.9): up to 10,000 files, a folder rename is atomic (Р2). Clarified by Т4: the client splits commits into 1,000 records and 1 MiB.

## Appendix В. Engine v1

Here the rules of the sync engine are fixed before the code (section 6, Э3, step 0). The tests, the simulation (`sync/sim`) and the engine (`sync/core/src/engine/`) are written **to this appendix**. It refines Р2, Р3, Р7–Р10, Р13 and section 5 and does not cancel them; the protocol is appendix Б and the format is appendix А, and they are not repeated here. Each decision is a separate row with a number `Д…`; verified by Opus on October 21, 2026 (mark "verified by Opus, 21.10"; changes and additions from the review — Д56, Д57, Д13, Д70–Д72 and section В.9). If a decision changes, the tests and the engine change in one separate commit after review. Open questions are at the end of the appendix (В.9).

### В.0. Terms and design

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Mark                    |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д1  | **Terms.** A _record_ is a file or folder with an `item_id` (Р2). _Local_ is what is on disk right now. _Server_ is the latest version of a record in the feed (П31). A record's **base** is its _last common version_ (Р7, Р10): what the device last knew to be identical both on disk and on the server; in the state (Д3) it is the path, kind, hash, size, `mtime_ns`, version number and `body_id`. The base changes **only** in four cases: (a) a file was written to disk from the server's version; (b) our commit was accepted by the server (including recognized in the feed by hash, С2); (c) the local and the server versions turned out identical (path and hash) and were linked without transferring data (Д15); (d) a deletion was confirmed on both sides. The state "changed locally" is a divergence from the base, and "changed on the server" is a server version newer than the base. | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д2  | **Ports.** The engine is a single function `sync_cycle` (one pass). It sees the world only through five ports: `LocalFs`, `Remote`, `StateStore`, `Clock` and `Random` (Р12). There are no threads, no `async` and no global state; all calls go in sequence, so the simulation is reproducible from a seed (section 7). Time (`Clock`) goes only into list timestamps (conflicts, "not synced") and **never** into decisions (Р3, С10). The ports know nothing about Tauri, SQLite or the network: the real implementations live in the app (Э4).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.1. Device state (Р10)

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Mark                    |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- |
| Д3  | **What is stored.** The server's `instance_id`; `cursor` and `head` (Д5); for each record: `item_id`, kind, path (NFC), hash, size, `mtime_ns`, version number, `body_id`, the "deleted" flag, the "format is newer" flag (Д66) and unknown header fields (Д67); unfinished uploads (Д6); at most one unfinished commit (Д7); the list of conflicts (Д47); the "not synced" list (Д64). The device identifier, the server address, the space ID and the keys are the app's concern (Э4), the engine does not store them. The entry of a deleted file (a _tombstone_) stays in the state forever: it is needed to notice a rollback of a deleted record (Д57) and not to revive it by re-reading the feed.                                                                                                          | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д4  | **Atomicity and order.** Any change of state is a single `StateTx` transaction (all or nothing). In each step, first the action in the world (a file on disk, a server response), then the state transaction. A crash between them leaves the world ahead of the state; the next pass sees this on its own and creates no conflict, because a local version equal to the server's is simply linked (Д15). Therefore **every step is idempotent**: a repeat after a crash at any point arrives at the same result (С3). The engine keeps nothing between passes that is not in the state.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д5  | **Cursor.** `cursor` advances to `seq` N only if **all** feed records with `seq ≤ N` have been handled: applied, linked, recorded as a conflict, "not synced" or "read-only". A record that could not be handled (disk error, corrupted data) holds the cursor in front of it: the pass will repeat it, and the others are applied as usual (a repeat is safe, Д4). `head` is the largest `seq` the server has named to this device. The cursor is the number given by the server (`next`), the engine does not make it up.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д6  | **Unfinished upload (С1).** Before an upload of a body starts, `{blob_id, item_id, content_hash, size, nonce prefix (19 bytes)}` is written to the state. After an interruption the pass reads the file again; if the hash is the same, it **encrypts again with the same prefix** (STREAM with the same key, prefix and content gives the same bytes) and uploads the parts that are missing (`blob_start` for a known body answers 200 with a list). If the file has changed, the upload record is discarded and the body is uploaded anew with a new `blob_id` (an abandoned one will be cleaned up by the server after 24 hours, П25). The upload record is deleted in the same transaction that accepts the commit (Д7).                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д7  | **Unfinished commit (С2).** Before sending, the batch is written to the state in full: `commit_id`, the records (`item_id`, `base_version`, header envelope, `blob_id`, `deleted`) and what will become the base after success. After a crash the pass sends **the same** batch with the same `commit_id`, without re-encrypting the headers (otherwise the batch content would change, `commit_id_reused`). A response "accepted" or "replay" — the base is updated. `409 version_conflict` — the batch was not applied: its record is deleted, and the records go through decisions again (Д16) with the new server version. If the response is lost but the commit arrived, a replay returns the earlier result (П28); if the batch record is lost as well, the commit is recognized in the feed by hash (Д15). | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д8  | **No state** (lost, or the vault was copied with `.amby/` to another computer, С11, Р10) — this is a **full reconciliation without deletions**. The feed is read from zero; a file whose path and hash match is linked; a matching path but a different hash is a "both created" conflict (Д43); a file only on the server is downloaded; a file only on disk is uploaded as a new record (Д45). Nothing is deleted anywhere: neither on disk nor on the server. The same reconciliation is started by a feed gap (Д58) and by the agreement after a server reset (Д56).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д9  | **Lock (С14).** The first action of a pass is `StateStore::lock`; the lock is held to the end. If it is taken — `EngineError::Locked`: nothing has been read or written. Releasing the lock when the process crashes is the concern of the port implementation (in the simulation the lock is released when the device is recreated).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.2. The pass

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | Mark                    |
| --- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д10 | **Order of steps.** 0 — lock (Д9); 1 — cleanup of unfinished temporary files (`cleanup_staged`); 2 — `info`: protocol versions and `instance_id` (Д56); 3 — local scan (Д12, Д13); 4 — reading the feed (Д14); 5 — guards (Д54–Д58): if any of them triggers, the pass **stops without applying or sending anything**; 6 — decisions on **all** records (Д16), with no writes to disk; 7 — applying incoming changes (Д17); 8 — outgoing: bodies, then commits (Д18); 9 — report (Д20). If a commit gets `version_conflict`, the pass repeats steps 3–8, at most 5 times; after that the result is `Busy` (someone is committing faster; retry on the next pass). Decisions on all records are made before the first action: this way the mass-change guard sees the full picture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д11 | **What is not synced (Р4).** The engine decides by path, not by settings: `.git` at any depth; `.obsidian/` and `.trash/` in the root; everything in `.amby/` except `properties.json`, `blocks/`, `graph-layout.json`, `property-backups/`; temporary files `.<name>.amby-tmp-*`; system junk (`.DS_Store`, `._*`, `Thumbs.db`, `desktop.ini`, by name at any depth). These paths are silently excluded. Separately, **with an entry in the "not synced" list** (Д64): symbolic links (`Symlink`), names not in UTF-8 (`NotUtf8`), files whose encrypted size is larger than `max_blob_bytes` (`TooLarge`). An incoming record with an excluded path is not written to disk and also goes into the list (`ExcludedPath`): another client sent it, but the rule is ours.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д12 | **Scan (Р10).** `list` returns the path, kind, size and `mtime_ns`. A file with a record whose size and `mtime_ns` equal the base is considered unchanged without reading; otherwise it is read and BLAKE3 is computed. The size and `mtime_ns` values are taken from `list` **before** reading: a file that changed during reading will get the old values in the base and will be read again on the next pass (С9). The header and the body are built from the same bytes that were read.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д13 | **Recognizing our own renames.** A record with a base whose file has disappeared, and a new file without a record with **the same hash and size**, is a rename if the pair is unique: exactly one disappeared record and exactly one new file with this hash. Otherwise it is a deletion and a creation (safe: nothing is lost, on other devices the old file will go to the trash and a new one will appear). A folder: a disappeared folder with a record and a new folder without a record is a rename if the set of "relative path, hash" pairs of files with records inside the disappeared folder matches the set of files inside the new one; then the files inside are renamed in pairs by relative path (identical files are not confused). A rename **together with** an edit is a deletion and a creation (the hash is different). A change of case only (`Note.md` → `note.md`) is a rename. **App hint (Д70):** a rename together with an edit inside Amby is not turned into a deletion and a creation if the app passed the engine a hint. | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д14 | **Feed.** Pages are read from the cursor until `more = false` (in a full reconciliation — from zero). Each header is opened with `open_header`. Errors `Authentication`, `VersionMismatch`, `EpochMismatch`, `Malformed`, `BadPadding`, `InvalidPath`, `PathNotNfc` (С5): in the same pass the page is requested one more time; if the error remains, the record is skipped, the error goes into the report, the cursor is held (Д5), and nothing is written to disk. `UnsupportedFormat` and `UnsupportedVersion` are "read-only" (Д66).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д15 | **Linking.** If the local version equals the server's by path and hash (or a deleted record corresponds to the absence of a file), nothing is written to disk and nothing is sent: the base is updated to the server version. One rule covers recognizing our own commit in the feed (С2), recovery after a crash (Д4), reconciliation without state (Д8) and two identical files created on two devices. For folders, linking is a path match.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д16 | **The decision on a record** is a function of three inputs (base, local, server). Its result is one of the rows of table В.3. The local version is looked up by the base path, then by the pair from Д13, then by the server path (this is how files already moved by a parent folder are found).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д17 | **Applying incoming changes (step 7).** Order: (1) folders by ascending depth; (2) file renames; (3) creations and overwrites; (4) deletions; (5) `remove_empty_dir` for folders that have become unnecessary. The whole step sits between `batch_begin` and `batch_end` (Р8), and `batch_end` is called on error too. An overwrite always goes through `begin_write`, `StagedWrite::publish` (a temporary file next to the target, atomic publish) and with a `SyncOverwrite` snapshot (Д53). Before a deletion or an overwrite the file's hash is checked once more: if it no longer equals the base, the action is not performed, and the record will return on the next pass (С9). An error on one record (disk full, С4, forbidden) does not interrupt the pass: it goes into the report, the cursor is held, the file is untouched. Loss of the network interrupts the pass (`EngineError::Remote`), the state is intact.                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д18 | **Outgoing (step 8).** For a creation and an edit the body is uploaded first (Д6); for a rename and a deletion no body is needed (a rename carries the earlier `body_id` and hash, П30). Then the commits, at most `max_commit_items` records. A body is encrypted and read entirely in memory; therefore the device has a file size limit (Д71). The folder rename batch (the folder record and all its files) goes in **one** commit if there are no more than `max_commit_items` records; if more, in several, first the folder record, then the files by path (Р2, Д50). The rest is split in path order. While the log of a multi-file operation is unfinished, step 8 is skipped entirely (Д59).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д19 | **Network.** `RemoteError::Network` interrupts the pass, nothing is retried within a pass (the exception is Д14): the retry is the next call. Behavior on an interruption depends only on the state (С1, С2), so the simulation can interrupt any request. `Unauthorized` and `Revoked` stop the pass with `Stopped(AuthRequired)` (С12): nothing has been downloaded or written.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д20 | **Report.** `CycleReport`: the outcome (`Done`, `Stopped(reason)`, `Busy`); numbers (downloaded, uploaded, renamed, sent to trash); created conflicts; the "not synced" list; "read-only" records and the `needs_update` flag; per-record errors; `outgoing_paused`; `full_reconcile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.3. The Р7 table as rules

Notation. _Local_: `=` as in the base; `E` content changed (at the base path); `M` moved, content the same (Д13); `X` deleted. _Server_: `=` same version; `C` content changed, same path; `M` renamed, same content; `CM` both; `D` deleted. "Copy" is a conflict copy (Д46). "Uploaded" is what goes to the server in step 8. The base after a row is the server version unless stated otherwise. For folders the same rows apply without the words about content: a folder has no `E`, `C`, `CM`, download or body; a folder is deleted only when no files are left in it (Д17, step 5), otherwise it stays, and a local folder without a record later becomes a new record (Д45).

| №   | Local   | Server | Result                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Uploaded                                                                            | Mark                    |
| --- | ------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д21 | `=`     | `=`    | nothing                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д22 | `E`     | `=`    | own edit goes out                                                                                                                                                                                                            | new body; record: same path, new hash, size, `body_id`; `base_version` = base       | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д23 | `M`     | `=`    | own rename goes out                                                                                                                                                                                                          | only the header with the new path, earlier `body_id` and hash                       | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д24 | `X`     | `=`    | own deletion goes out (outgoing guard, Д54)                                                                                                                                                                                  | record `deleted: true`, no body; the base is a tombstone                            | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д25 | `=`     | `C`    | download, verify, `SyncOverwrite` snapshot, replace the file                                                                                                                                                                 | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д26 | `=`     | `M`    | the file is renamed on disk to the server's path (missing folders are created)                                                                                                                                               | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д27 | `=`     | `CM`   | rename, then download and replace with a snapshot                                                                                                                                                                            | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д28 | `=`     | `D`    | file to the vault trash (Д52), tombstone (incoming guard, Д54)                                                                                                                                                               | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д29 | `E`     | `C`    | **Р7, row 1.** The server version's hash equals the local one — link (Д15). Otherwise: a copy of the local content next to it, then the file is replaced by the server's version with a snapshot                             | copy as a new record (Д47); the original is not uploaded                            | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д30 | `E`     | `M`    | **Р7, row 2.** The file is renamed to the server's path, the content stays our own                                                                                                                                           | edit: server path, new body, `base_version` = the server's                          | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д31 | `E`     | `CM`   | like Д29, but at the server's path: at the server's path the server's version, the copy next to it                                                                                                                           | copy as a new record                                                                | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д32 | `E`     | `D`    | **Р7, row 4.** The edit wins: the file stays                                                                                                                                                                                 | a new version of the same record: `deleted: false`, local path, new body            | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д33 | `M`     | `C`    | **Р7, row 2.** The name stays our own, the content is taken from the server: the file under the new name is replaced by the server's version with a snapshot                                                                 | record: local path, the server's hash, size and `body_id`, the body is not uploaded | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д34 | `M`     | `M`    | **Р7, row 3.** The names match — link. They differ — the server's name stays: the file is renamed to the server's path                                                                                                       | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д35 | `M`     | `CM`   | the server's name (as Д34) and the server's content (as Д25)                                                                                                                                                                 | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д36 | `M`     | `D`    | a rename is a change (Д52): the file stays under its own name                                                                                                                                                                | a new version of the record: `deleted: false`, local path, **new** body             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д37 | `X`     | `C`    | **Р7, row 4.** The edit wins: the file is created anew at the server's path from the server's version                                                                                                                        | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д38 | `X`     | `M`    | a deletion does not beat someone else's change: the file is created at the server's path from the server's version                                                                                                           | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д39 | `X`     | `CM`   | same as Д38                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д40 | `X`     | `D`    | both deleted: tombstone, the base is the server version                                                                                                                                                                      | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д41 | no base | record | the server record is new, locally empty at its path: the file (folder) is created                                                                                                                                            | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д42 | no base | record | at its path there is a local file with the same hash (or a folder): link (Д15, Д8)                                                                                                                                           | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д43 | no base | record | **Р7, row 5.** At its path there is a local file with a different hash: the "second" is the one that reached the server later, that is, the local one: a copy of the local, at the path the server's version with a snapshot | copy as a new record                                                                | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д44 | no base | `D`    | the server record is deleted, there is no base: tombstone; a local file at its path, if any, does not belong to this record                                                                                                  | nothing                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д45 | new     | —      | a local file or folder without a record (neither in the base nor in the feed at this path): a new record                                                                                                                     | body; record with a new `item_id`, `base_version = 0`                               | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | Mark                    |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д46 | **Conflict copy name.** `Name.<id>-conflict.ext` in the same folder as the original's final path (at the server's path for Д31 and Д43). `Name` and `ext` are split at the last dot (`a.b.md` → `a.b.<id>-conflict.md`); without an extension — `Name.<id>-conflict`; a name made of one dot and letters (`.env`) counts entirely as `Name`. `<id>` is 12 lowercase hex characters: the first 6 bytes of `BLAKE3("amby-sync/conflict/1" ‖ item_id ‖ hash of the local content ‖ server version number (8 bytes, big-endian))`. It is **deterministic**: a repeat after a crash gives the same name and does not breed copies (Д4). If the place is occupied by a file with the same content — the copy already exists, a second one is not created; if occupied by different content — the next `id` (the counter is part of the hash). If the copy's path is occupied by a _server record_ with the same content (another participant has already made the same decision and their copy reached the server), the copy is not created: it arrives by itself. The copy goes through the same name checks as any file (Д62). For row Д43 the server's `item_id` is used. | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д47 | **A conflict copy in the list and on the server.** The copy is first created on disk, then written to the list of conflicts (`ConflictRecord`: `id`, kind, original path, copy path, `created_ms` by `Clock`), then the original is replaced. In the same pass the copy is uploaded as an ordinary creation (Д45) and appears on all devices. A conflict stays in the list until it is resolved (the Э4 interface); the engine deletes it only on `resolve_conflict`. The overwritten version of the original also remains as a snapshot (Д53).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.4. Renames and occupied paths

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Mark                    |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д48 | **Our own rename of a file** is a record with a new `path`, `version = base + 1`, the earlier `content_hash`, `size`, `body_id`; the body is not uploaded (Д23). While the old version is alive on the server, its body is too (П35).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д49 | **Someone else's rename and an occupied target.** The file is moved by `LocalFs::rename`, which overwrites nothing. If the target is occupied by a file _without a record_ — this is Д43: that file becomes a conflict copy, and the server's version stays at the path. If the target is occupied by a file of _another record_ that itself leaves this path in the same pass (a swap of names, a chain) — the move is postponed until the target is free; at most three rounds within the step, and the remainder goes to "not synced" with the reason `PathBusy` and will be repeated on the next pass. Missing parent folders are created.                                                                                                                                                                                | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д50 | **Folder rename.** Locally: the folder record with the new path and the records of all its files with new paths — one atomic commit (Р2) up to `max_commit_items` records; if more — several commits: first the folder record, then the files in portions by path. The receiver survives any order: each record is applied on its own (a file is moved by its record), folders are not renamed as a whole, the empty old folder is removed at the end (Д17, step 5), missing folders are created on write. So the intermediate state with 10,000+ commits is visible but breaks nothing, and a repeat after a crash is safe. The receiver does not apply a batch halfway: a commit arrives as a whole page (П31). Local files without a record that ended up in the old folder stay in it and later become new records (Д45). | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д51 | **Writing to an occupied path in general.** Any incoming record (creation, move, copy) to a path occupied by a local file without a record acts as Д43. If occupied by a file of the same record — it is an overwrite per Д25.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.5. Deletion and safeguards (Р8)

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | Note                    |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д52 | **Deletion from another device.** Performed only if the local file equals the base (row Д28) — the hash is re-checked right before the action (Д17). Deletion is always `move_to_trash(…, RemoteDeletion)`: `LocalFs` has no other way to delete a file. A modified file is not deleted (rows Д32, Д36). A rename counts as a modification, because the user touched the file.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д53 | **Snapshot before overwriting.** Any replacement of an existing local file (Д25, Д27, Д29–Д31, Д33, Д35, Д43) goes through `StagedWrite::publish(Some(SyncOverwrite))`: the port must save the previous content to history before the replacement, even if history is turned off. Writing a new file needs no snapshot (`None`). A snapshot is also taken for a file replaced by a conflict copy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д54 | **Mass changes.** Two quantities are counted. _Incoming_ — the number of existing local files that the pass will replace (any replacement from Д53) or move to the trash. _Outgoing_ — the number of local deletions that the pass would send (row Д24). Creations and renames are not counted. The denominator is the number of files with a live record in the base at the start of the pass (at least 1). Threshold: `N ≥ 20` **and** `N · 10 > denominator` (more than 10%): 20 of 200 — goes through, 21 of 200 and 20 of 199 — stop, 19 of 50 — goes through. The quantities are checked separately. A stop is `Stopped(MassChange{direction, list})`: the whole pass, without exceptions, applies and sends nothing. The consent `approvals.mass_change` lifts this safeguard for one pass and does not cancel the others.                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д55 | **Missing or empty vault folder (С8).** The root is absent (`root_state = Missing`) — `Stopped(VaultMissing)`: it cannot be confirmed, nothing is done. The root exists, but contains no synchronizable entries at all while the base has live files — `Stopped(VaultEmpty)`; the consent `approvals.accept_empty_vault` allows the pass, and deletions from it continue through Д54. A disappearance is never treated as a deletion.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д56 | **Server reset (С7).** The first pass remembers `instance_id`. After that: (a) `info.instance_id` differs — `Stopped(ServerReset)`; (b) the feed head (`seq`) is less than the cursor — `Stopped(ServerBehind)` (the server was restored from an old copy). Stop before reading records: local files are untouched, **an empty or short response is never a command to delete**. Consent `approvals.resync` ("upload the vault anew from this device"): the record state, cursor, `head`, and anything unfinished are erased, the new `instance_id` is remembered, and the pass runs as a full reconciliation without deletions (Д8) with one difference from Д43: if different contents lie at the same path, **the local version stays at the path** and goes to the server as a new version of the record, while the differing server version is saved as a **conflict copy** (Д46), because in these cases the local version is almost always newer. Nothing is lost in either variant. The conflict lists and the "not synchronized" list are kept. | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д57 | **Record version rollback (Р6, С6).** A feed record whose version is lower than the version in the base is a rollback; the same version with a different path, hash, `body_id`, or deletion mark is one too. Checked for all records (including tombstones) before anything is applied: `Stopped(Rollback{item_id, known, received})`, nothing is written to disk or to the state. The way out is the same `approvals.resync`, with the same rule: the local version stays at the path, the server one becomes a copy (Д56).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д58 | **Feed gap (П32).** A first page with `gap = true` triggers a full reconciliation **without deletions**: the cursor is discarded, the feed is read from scratch, and everything proceeds as in Д8. A base record that is absent from the full feed is discarded, while the file stays and later becomes a new record (Д45). A server-side deletion of a record with a live base does not delete the file: the record is discarded, the file stays (and comes back to life as new). `CycleReport.full_reconcile = true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д59 | **Unfinished multi-file journals.** `recovery_pending() = true` — step 8 is skipped entirely (no bodies, no commits), incoming changes are applied, `outgoing_paused = true`. This is not an error; on the next pass without a journal the outgoing changes are sent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д60 | **Stops and consents.** Reasons: `MassChange`, `VaultMissing`, `VaultEmpty`, `ServerReset`, `ServerBehind`, `Rollback`, `AuthRequired`, `IncompatibleServer` (protocol versions outside `[1,1]`). Consents (`Approvals`): `mass_change`, `accept_empty_vault`, `resync`; they apply to one pass and are passed in by the app after the user's answer. A stopped pass returns `Ok` with the outcome `Stopped`, not an error; the state does not change in that case, except for remembering the first `instance_id`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.6. Names across systems (Р9)

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Note                    |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- |
| Д61 | **NFC.** The comparison key and the path in the header are NFC. A local name may be NFD (macOS): the engine builds an NFC key, stores NFC in the base, and on disk uses the spelling that `list` returned for this record (if there is no file — NFC). Two local names with the same NFC key (possible on Linux) — the second one in byte order goes to "not synchronized" (`NameCollision`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д62 | **System rules.** Before writing a name to disk (creation, move target, conflict copy), `check_path_for_system(path, rules)`. Violations (`NameProblem`): a forbidden character `<>:"\|?*` with `windows_names`; a segment ends with a dot or space with `windows_names`; a segment is a reserved Windows name (`CON`, `PRN`, `AUX`, `NUL`, `COM1`–`COM9`, `LPT1`–`LPT9`, case-insensitive, with or without an extension: `con.txt` too) with `windows_names`; a segment longer than `max_segment_bytes`; a path longer than `max_path_bytes` (in UTF-8 bytes). Sets `NameRules::windows()` (255/259, case-insensitive), `macos()` (255/1024, case-insensitive), `linux()` (255/4096, case-sensitive). Cannot be created — the file does not appear here, it goes to "not synchronized" (`InvalidName`), stays on the server and other devices, no silent renames. | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д63 | **Case.** With `case_insensitive`, two names with the same `lowercase(NFC)` key cannot coexist. What is already on disk or in the base stays; the one that arrived second is not written and goes to "not synchronized" (`CaseCollision{with which})`; on the server it stays untouched. Without `case_insensitive`, `Note.md` and `note.md` are two different files.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д64 | **The "not synchronized" list.** `UnsyncedRecord{path, reason, detail}`; reasons: `Symlink`, `NotUtf8`, `TooLarge`, `ExcludedPath`, `InvalidName(NameProblem)`, `CaseCollision`, `NameCollision`, `PathBusy`. The list is rebuilt in full on every pass (a record disappears when its reason disappears).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.7. Header, new formats, streaming body opening

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Note                    |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------- |
| Д65 | **Field consistency** (the Э1 core checks only the kind, Ф14). Only four combinations are valid: a file that is not deleted — has `body_id` and `content_hash`; a deleted file — `body_id` and `content_hash` are empty, `size = 0`; a folder (deleted or not) — `body_id` and `content_hash` are empty, `size = 0`. In addition: `format = 1`, `version ≥ 1`. A feed record, compared with the header: the record's `blob_id` equals the header's `body_id` (both empty or both equal); if there is a body, `blob_size` equals `sealed_body_len(size)`. The open `deleted` mark of the feed record is not compared: the header is trusted (Р6). A violation is the same as forgery (Д14). The `kind` of an existing record does not change: a different `kind` in a new version is a violation.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д66 | **Newer format (Р13, С13).** `UnsupportedFormat` or `UnsupportedVersion` when opening the header — the record is "read-only": it is counted in the report (`read_only`, `needs_update = true`), the cursor passes over it, it is **not written, renamed, or deleted** on disk, and local edits of such a file are not sent (we do not rewrite other people's records). The other records are applied as usual. The path of such a record is unknown if we have not seen it before.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д67 | **Unknown header fields** (Р13) are remembered in the state together with the record and are included in every header that this device writes for it (edit, rename, deletion).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д68 | **Streaming body opening (Ф24).** `open_body_stream(vk, body_id, size, content_hash, source: Read, sink: Write)` reads the body in chunks and writes the **content** (without padding) to the sink. The sink is a temporary file next to the target (`LocalFs::begin_write`); publication (`StagedWrite::publish`) is called by the caller **only after `Ok`**, that is, once the authenticity of all chunks and the last chunk, the length `padme(size)`, the zeros after the content, and the BLAKE3 have been verified. On any error the caller does `abort`: the previous file stays in place of the target. Memory is bounded: no more than two chunks (about 128 KiB) plus a constant, regardless of file size; no more than 64 KiB goes to the sink at a time. For the same input the result matches `open_body` from Э1 (where that returns `Ok`, the content is the same; where it errors, the kind of error is the same). The source of I/O errors (a read interruption) is a separate error kind, not an authenticity error (С1 differs from С5). | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.8. Failures (section 5) as engine rules

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Note                    |
| --- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д69 | **Table.** С1: Д6 (resume, same body). С2: Д7 and Д15. С3: Д4 and Д17 (the temporary file is not published until verified; the remainder is removed by `cleanup_staged`; on disk there is either the old or the new file, whole). С4: `FsError::NoSpace` from any write — the file is untouched, the write error is in the report, the cursor holds (Д17). С5: Д14 and Д68. С6: Д57. С7: Д56. С8: Д55. С9: Д12 and Д17. С10: the clock does not affect decisions (Д2). С11: Д8. С12: Д19 (`AuthRequired`, nothing downloaded). С13: Д66. С14: Д9. | verified by Opus, 21.10 |

### В.9. Opus decisions (October 21)

1. Д46 accepted: a deterministic `id` for the copy.
2. Д13 accepted. The app knows its own renames (the Amby rename and move commands) and passes the engine a "this is a rename" hint (Д70, Э4), so that a rename together with an edit inside Amby does not turn into a deletion and a creation.
3. Д36 and Д52 accepted: a rename wins over another device's deletion, this is safer.
4. Д54 accepted: stop the whole pass.
5. Д56 and Д57 changed: with `resync` the local version stays at the path, the server one is saved as a conflict copy.
6. Д58 accepted: coming back to life is safe.
7. Д6, Д12, Д18: streaming body encryption is deferred to Э4 (Д71): until it appears, files larger than the device's memory limit go to "not synchronized" with a clear reason.
8. Д50, Д3, Д62, Д65, Д66 accepted.
9. The reset model is sufficient; a real case has been added (Д72).
10. Д19 accepted: the app repeats passes with a growing pause (Э4).

### В.10. Additions after review

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | Note                    |
| --- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- |
| Д70 | **Rename hint.** `CycleRequest.rename_hints` — a list of "from, to" pairs (paths in NFC) that the app composed from the Amby rename and move commands since the last pass. A hint is used once. If the file at the "from" path is in the base and has disappeared, and at the "to" path there is a file without a record, this is a rename, even if the content has changed (Д13): the record stays the same, the version is new, the path is new, the hash and body are new. A hint about a nonexistent file changes nothing. Without a hint, Д13 applies. **Race with a running pass:** the hint is recorded before the rename on disk, and the pass may have already started, so the engine takes the hints from the app (`LocalFs::take_rename_hints`) right after reading the disk, not at the start of the pass; a hint that the pass did not apply (it has not yet seen the rename) waits one more pass, no longer.                                                                                                                         | verified by Opus, 21.10 |
| Д71 | **Two live items at one path** (found by simulation, seed 2000522; Opus decision, 23.10).
