The pass result is `Done`, and in the report (Д20) `limits: {max_file_bytes, free_bytes, quota_full}` is added to `unsynced` for the interface.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | checked by Opus, 24.10 |

### Е.7. What changes in earlier decisions

| Was                                                                                                                        | Now                                                                                                                                                                        |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| П42: the quota includes the bodies of all live versions (latest and earlier), unfinished and deleted records until cleanup | П55: the quota includes the latest non-deleted versions and pending uploads; history and deleted records are counted separately (`history_bytes`) and are not in the quota |
| П42: checked only in `POST …/blobs`                                                                                        | П56, П60: also in `commit` (exactly, by growth) and with credit for replacement; a commit without growth always passes                                                     |
| П35, П36: the retention period is counted from `superseded_ms`/the deletion commit by the general setting at cleanup time  | П57-г: the period is fixed at replacement (`keep_until_ms`); without a value — by the previous rule                                                                        |
| П8: `blob_too_large` without details                                                                                       | П59: `details` with the size, the limit and the source                                                                                                                     |
| Д71 `UnsyncedReason::TooLarge` — "larger than the server accepts" (`info.max_blob_bytes`)                                  | Д74: the limit comes from `account()` (plan and ceiling), the `source` field; the `QuotaFull` reason is added                                                              |
| Б.9: `AMBY_SYNC_QUOTA_BYTES_PER_USER` — the only quota                                                                     | П54: the server ceiling; the plan may be even smaller                                                                                                                      |

Clients from before appendix Е see `507`/`413` from `commit` as an unknown error and will stop the pass; sync has not been released yet, so this is acceptable.

Added to table Б.0.1: `403 space_limit`; `413 blob_too_large` and `507 quota_exceeded` get `details`. Added to Б.9: `AMBY_SYNC_PLANS_FILE`.

### Е.8. The app (У-decisions)

It shows the data from `GET /v1/account` (П61), decrypting names in Rust (`space.rs`, the space key from its `wrapped_vk`); the interface receives ready-made strings and never sees keys. All visible texts go in `src/locales/resources.ts` in all languages; error codes are translated in `sync.errors.*`.

| №   | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | Note                   |
| --- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------- |
| У1  | **A "Vaults of the account" section** («Хранилища аккаунта») in the sync settings (next to the device list), without a new module. A vault row: **name** (decrypted; if decryption failed — «Название недоступно» (Name unavailable)), **size** (`live_bytes`; `history_bytes` — a second line in gray, «и история …» ("and history …")), a **«Это хранилище»** (This vault) label (the space the open vault is linked to), **«Привязано на этом компьютере: <имя папки>»** (Linked on this computer: <folder name>) — for the other spaces, if among the vaults known to the app on this device there is one linked to that `space_id` (the name is the folder name, the path is not shown); otherwise «Подключено на других устройствах или нигде» (Connected on other devices or nowhere). Order: «Это хранилище» first, then by size. Refreshed when the section is opened, on the «Обновить» (Refresh) button, and at the end of a pass if a limit was reported; no timer polling.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | checked by Opus, 24.10 |
| У2  | **Section header.** Plan: «Тариф: Стандарт» (Plan: Standard) (`title`, otherwise the name; with no plan and no limits — «Свой сервер: без ограничений» (Own server: no limits)). Used: a "used / limit" gauge by `live_bytes + pending_bytes` and `max_total_bytes`, caption «312 МБ из 1 ГБ» (312 MB of 1 GB); from 80% — a warning color, from 100% — the error color. Limit rows only for the limits that are set: «Хранилищ: 1 из 1» (Vaults: 1 of 1), «Файл до 20 МБ» (File up to 20 MB), «История 30 дней, до 1 ГБ» (History 30 days, up to 1 GB). **Without limits** (`max_total_bytes = null`) — no gauge: «Занято 312 МБ» (Used 312 MB), no percentages. `history_bytes` — a separate row. Changing the plan is not in the app: the caption «Тариф меняет владелец сервера» (The plan is changed by the server owner) (there is no payment; a payment link will appear with payment). Sizes are decimal (as in Finder), with no conversion to binary.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | checked by Opus, 24.10 |
| У3  | **Three notices** (cards in the `sync-notices-host` stack, not pop-up windows; they disappear when the cause is gone; once per cause, no repeats on every pass). **File too large:** «N файлов не отправлены (размер каждого указан в списке): больше 20 МБ на вашем тарифе. Они остаются на этом компьютере, на других устройствах ничего не изменилось» (N files were not sent (the size of each is in the list): over 20 MB on your plan. They stay on this computer, nothing changed on other devices) + «Показать список» (Show list) (the «Не синхронизируется» (Not syncing) section with the reason and the limit). **Quota full:** «Хранилище заполнено. Новые и изменённые файлы не отправляются, файлы с других устройств приходят как обычно. Удалите ненужное или попросите владельца сервера увеличить тариф» (The vault is full. New and changed files are not sent, files from other devices arrive as usual. Delete what you do not need or ask the server owner to raise the plan). **Vault limit** (in the connection wizard, on "Create new"): «На вашем тарифе одно хранилище. Подключите существующее или обратитесь к владельцу сервера» (Your plan has one vault. Connect an existing one or contact the server owner) + a «Выбрать существующее» (Choose existing) button (the У1 list). On a server without limits there are no notices. None of this blocks working with notes. | checked by Opus, 24.10 |
| У4  | **Where space deletion will be (Э5).** In the vault row (У1) — a «Удалить с сервера…» (Delete from the server…) menu item **will be added in Э5** (confirmation by name, shows the size, irreversible; the server will receive `DELETE /v1/spaces/{id}`; until then — open question 3). The item does not exist now; room is left in the row markup, but we do not write Э5 code or texts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | checked by Opus, 24.10 |
| У5  | **The Rust side** (`src-tauri/src/sync/`): the command `sync_account_overview` (requests `account()`, decrypts the names, matches `space_id` with the vaults linked on the device), the type `AccountOverview` with Specta (the bindings in `bindings.ts` are regenerated, not edited by hand). The interface test port (`fake-sync-port.ts`) has the same data. In the browser build (`unavailable`) the section is hidden.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | checked by Opus, 24.10 |

### Е.9. Requirements the tests are written from (red, before the code)

For the owner tests: **two tests of the existing `sync/conformance/tests/quota.rs` change** — `old_live_versions_count_too` and `a_deleted_item_counts_until_cleanup` describe the earlier rule П42 and after П55 become "an earlier version does **not** count" and "a deleted record does not count"; the rest there stay.

| Check                                   | What it proves                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Where                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| The file and parsing of plans (П50–П52) | a valid file is read; an unknown field, a zero, a duplicate, a `default_plan` with no plan, a pinned unknown plan — startup refusal; no file — no limits; changing `default_plan` moves the unpinned; a pinned one does not move                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `sync/server` (unit), `sync/conformance/tests/plans.rs`                                                            |
| Commands (П53)                          | `user plan set/show`, `plans list/check`, `user add --plan`, `user list` with the plan and the used amount; exit codes; the plan takes effect without a restart                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `sync/conformance/tests/admin.rs`                                                                                  |
| Relation to the ceilings (П54)          | the smaller of the two applies for the file and the quota; history — by the plan, with no value — by the server setting                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `plans.rs`                                                                                                         |
| What is counted (П55)                   | an earlier version, a deleted record and its body are not included; one body under two versions (a rename) is counted once; a pending upload is included by its declared size; the sum is over all spaces; `history_bytes` separately                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `quota.rs`                                                                                                         |
| Not getting stuck (П56)                 | at the very limit: deletion frees space at once; replacement by a smaller or equal one passes with `replaces` and without growth; a growing commit — `507` and nothing changes; a batch without growth passes for a user over quota; a `replaces` hint for someone else's record gives no credit; `DELETE` of an abandoned body frees the bytes, for a body with a version — `409`, for someone else's — `404`                                                                                                                                                                        | `quota.rs`, `blobs.rs`                                                                                             |
| Downgrade (П57)                         | after a plan is reduced: nothing deleted in `blobs` or `versions`; a growing change is rejected, a shrinking one passes; more spaces than the limit — they work, no new one; versions replaced before the change live the promised period, those replaced after — the new one (`ManualClock`); a row without `keep_until_ms` is removed by the previous rule                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `plans.rs`, `retention.rs`                                                                                         |
| Migration (П58)                         | a version 1 database with data → version 2: the data is byte-for-byte the same (users, versions, bodies); the copy is created and opens; check before and after; a failure in the middle of migration leaves version 1; a repeated run does not repeat; the `last_migration` record; a previous database without plans behaves as before                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `sync/server` (unit `db.rs`), `durability.rs`                                                                      |
| Codes and points (П59, П60)             | `space_limit`, `blob_too_large` with the source, `quota_exceeded` with the numbers — at every check point; the order of checks; an existing space and `space_exists` are checked before the limit; other people's spaces have no influence                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `limits.rs`, `spaces.rs`, `commits.rs`                                                                             |
| Two races                               | two devices growing at the same time into the last slot — exactly one is accepted; a batch rejected with `507` leaves no traces (П27)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `commits.rs`                                                                                                       |
| `GET /v1/account` (П61)                 | the shape of the response; effective limits; without a file — the ceilings; the space sizes add up to the total; other people's data is not visible; without a token — `401`; `name_enc` and `wrapped_vk` do not change                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `plans.rs`, `spaces.rs`                                                                                            |
| Engine: scenarios (Д73–Д78)             | a file that is too large stays on disk, a list with the reason and the limit, nothing changed on the server; the limit grew — the file went out; quota full — outgoing bodies wait, incoming ones flow; space freed — the waiting one went out; one device filled it — another sees `QuotaFull` and loses nothing; `507` on one file does not stop the rest; `account()` unavailable — the previous behavior; a conflict with a copy that does not fit — the copy is on disk and in the set                                                                                           | `sync/core/tests` (engine), `session_tests`                                                                        |
| Engine: simulation                      | the server model in `sync/sim` gets the same limits; random runs turn on plans, a downgrade in the middle of work and quota filling; **invariants:** (1) no local file disappears or changes because of a limit; (2) no version on the server is deleted because of a limit (until the cleanup deadline); (3) after the limit is lifted all files converge; (4) a refusal by limit is not counted as a mass change; (5) the quota is not exceeded by growth the server accepted; (6) failures on every call (`blob_start`, `blob_delete`, `commit`) do not lose the record of sending | `sync/sim`                                                                                                         |
| App: Rust (У5)                          | `sync_account_overview` on the test server: names decrypted; matching with linked vaults; a server without `account` — the "no limits" section; a decryption error on one name does not break the list                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `src-tauri/src/sync/*_tests.rs`                                                                                    |
| App: vitest (У1–У4)                     | the list (the «Это хранилище» label, linking, order, sizes); the gauge with the 80/100% thresholds; without limits — no gauge; three cards, their disappearance and absence of repeats; the wizard on `space_limit` offers existing ones; all keys exist in all languages; there is no delete item                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `src/components/workspace/sync/*.test.tsx`                                                                         |
| History ceiling (П50, П57-д)            | `history_bytes` above the ceiling: the oldest earlier versions and deleted records are removed, in order of replacement time; the latest version is never removed, the body of the latest version stays; below the ceiling — nothing; without a ceiling — as before; `ManualClock`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `retention.rs`, `plans.rs`, `sync/sim` (early cleanup does not touch the latest version, the "oldest first" order) |
| Space administration (П53)              | `space list` shows sizes, names are encrypted; `space remove` without `--yes` — refusal, with it — the space, versions and bodies disappear, other people's do not; no secrets in the log; `user plan set` rejects an unknown plan; `plans check` lists the pinned ones without a plan                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `admin.rs`                                                                                                         |
| Credit and different users (П55, П56)   | the same `blob_id` for two users and two spaces is not merged; `replaces` of someone else's record and of a record in another space gives no credit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `quota.rs`                                                                                                         |
| Remainder per pass (Д75)                | with several files the pass subtracts what was sent from `free_bytes`: it does not send ones that obviously will not fit; the server does not receive extra `blob_start`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | engine (`sync/core/tests`), simulation                                                                             |
| Server version (П62)                    | `server_version` is not `0.0.0` and equals the crate version                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `info.rs`                                                                                                          |
| Quota measurement (Е.10-7)              | the quota check with 100,000 versions takes no longer than 50 ms; does not block the build                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `sync/conformance` (a separate measurement)                                                                        |

### Е.10. Opus decisions on the open questions (October 24)

1. **The history ceiling** was accepted in a different form than "only in view": the hosted server's disk is 80 GB, and edits of 20 MB files without a ceiling grow history without limit. `max_history_bytes` is added (П50), with early cleanup of the oldest earlier versions and deleted records (П57-д); the latest versions are not touched. An own server without a value does not change.
2. **Restore when the quota is full** accepted as written (П56-в): the restore interface must say that the space is full and what to do.
3. **The space limit before Э5:** `max_spaces` stays; the administrator commands `space list` and `space remove` are added at once (П53). There is no delete button in the app before Э5 (У4).
4. **The pinned retention period (П57-г)** accepted; the only exception is the history ceiling (П57-д).
5. **Startup refusal on an unknown pinned plan** kept; `user plan set` rejects an unknown plan, `plans check` lists users without a plan in the file (П52).
6. **`replaces`** kept (П56-б); someone else's record gives no credit.
7. **Counting without counters** accepted: measure first. The goal of the measurement is no more than 50 ms per quota check with 100,000 versions (a check in `sync/conformance`, a separate measuring test, does not block the build; on failure — per-space counters and a `quota recount` command).
8. **Reloading the file on `SIGHUP`** is not needed now.
9. **Units are decimal everywhere** (П50): 1 GB = 1,000,000,000, 20 MB = 20,000,000; the app shows them like Finder. The notice about a file that is too large shows the size of the file itself.
10. **Pending bodies in the quota** accepted as written (П55).

Check additions: within a pass the engine subtracts what was sent from `free_bytes` (Д75, an engine test is mandatory); `(space, blob_id)` is not merged between users and spaces (П55); `/v1/info.server_version` is currently `0.0.0` — it now returns the crate version, the sync server crate is bumped together with this work (for example, 0.2.0; the protocol stays 1), a conformance suite test: the version is not `0.0.0` (П62).

## Appendix Ж. Merging `properties.json`

> **Replaced by appendix З (November 4, 2026).** Properties move: values go into the note header, presentation into 256 group files merged per note. From Ж the following remain in force: the base (Ж3, Ж29), protection against mass loss (Ж21), the unreadable side (Ж26) and the single fallback path (Ж28); the rest is the history of the decision.

Here the rules for merging `.amby/properties.json` by key are fixed before the code (section 6, strict order: this record → Opus review → a separate session of red tests → a separate session of code with the tests frozen). The owner's decision of October 24, 2026: the merge is done **before** the final run of the gates (mutation testing and the long simulation) described in stage Э4, and after appendix Е. The appendix continues appendix Г (К1 — the merge, К2 — the conflict store) and uses its base, the conflict set and the port. Each decision is a separate `Ж…` item. What it **replaces** is listed in Ж.7. The Opus decisions on the questions are in Ж.9. The appendix was reviewed by Opus on October 24, 2026; the review corrections are incorporated into Ж8, Ж20, Ж26, Ж27, Ж29, Ж30. Units are decimal, as in appendix Е.

### Ж.0. What exists now (from the code, 24.10)

- **The file.** `.amby/properties.json`, written only by `src-tauri/src/property_store.rs`. Format version 1:

  ```json
  {
    "version": 1,
    "notes": {
      "<note amby-id>": [
        {
          "id": "<ULID>",
          "name": "…",
          "icon": "…",
          "propertyType": "…",
          "value": "…",
          "settings": "…"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
  ```

  All six fields of a property are strings. `notes` is a dictionary "note `amby-id` → ordered list of properties"; the list order is the order in the properties panel (`reorder`). A property name is unique within a note, ASCII case-insensitively (`upsert` looks up by `id` **or** by name). A note without properties is not stored in the dictionary (`delete`, `backup_and_clear` remove the empty key).

- **What here is user data and what is derived.** User data is the whole content of the file: a note's set of properties, their `name`, `icon`, `propertyType`, `value`, `settings` and the order. Derived are the `note_custom_properties` table in SQLite (`restore_cache` builds it from the file) and the mirror in the note's YAML header (`Name: value`, written by `upsert`, `delete`). The mirror is not a source: the app reads the property list from the file (`property_store::list` through the cache), while the note header is separate note data, merged by Г11.
- **How the app writes (important for the merge).** `serde_json::to_string_pretty` over a `HashMap`: 2-space indent, a newline at the end, the order of the `notes` keys is **random** (it changes from write to write), while the order of a property's fields is fixed by the struct. The write goes through `frontmatter::atomic_write`, which preserves the BOM and the prevailing line endings of the previous file. Extra fields are **silently dropped** on read and disappear on the next write (`#[serde]` without `flatten`). A version other than 1 is a read error, and the file is not overwritten; an unreadable file is not overwritten either (test `malformed_sidecar_is_not_overwritten`).
- **What the engine does now.** The path `.amby/properties.json` is synced (Р4, `rules.rs`), but does not fit Г5 (it is inside `.amby/`), gets no base (Г6), and when edited on two devices (Д29, Д31) goes into a conflict set (Г20): the second version lies in `.amby/conflicts/<id>/other.json` and is visible on all devices. The decision "Replace with the copy" («Заменить копией»), which Э4 (23.10) spoke of, concerned a copy alongside that was local; with К2 that is gone. **What remains:** any simultaneous change to the properties of two notes is a conflict of the whole file, and it is resolved by choosing the whole file ("mine"/"other"): either the properties of all notes edited on one of the devices are lost, or a person has to compare two JSONs by hand.

### Ж.1. Which files the appendix covers

- **Ж1. Only `.amby/properties.json`.** The merge is enabled for one path: exactly `.amby/properties.json`, the name is case-sensitive (as in `rules.rs`). The rest is as it was.
- **Ж2. The other synced files of `.amby/` are not covered.**
  - `blocks/<note-id>.json` — one file per note (UI blocks), edited together with that note; a conflict happens at the same time as the note's own, and is shown together with it. It stays a set conflict (Г5).
  - `graph-layout.json` — node positions and the camera of the pinned graph; two devices edit it rarely and as a whole (a person moves the graph). It stays a conflict: the loss is only the layout, and "mine"/"other" is enough. A per-node merge is possible later (question Ж.9-8).
  - `property-backups/<note-id>/<operation-id>.json` — immutable files with unique names (ULID): two devices do not write the same path, there is no conflict, no merge is needed.
  - `conflicts/` — sets whose content is computed (Г19): there are no conflicts.
  - Database manifests (`<Название>.json` (`<Name>.json`), `ambd.json`) and `.ambd/` lie **not in `.amby/`** but in the user's tree, and are subject to Г5 ("JSON is not merged automatically"). The manifest format is written by another module and is more complex (schema, order of views, links to shards); a separate merge will be needed only on data about conflict frequency and in a separate appendix. They stay a set conflict.

### Ж.2. Merge by keys (three-way, against the base)

- **Ж3. The base — as in К1, an extension of Г5/Г6/Г8.** The file `.amby/properties.json` gets a **base** on the same terms as a note: the bytes of the last common version in `.amby/sync/bases/` through `StateStore` (Г7), key `(item_id, hash)`, updated in the same four cases of Г8, write order Г9, cleanup Г9. The size limit for this path is its own: no more than `PROPS_MERGE_MAX_BYTES` = 16,000,000 bytes (decimal) (Ж27). The content limit is Ж4. No base — a conflict (Г10), with no "merge from two versions".
- **Ж4. Admission (when merging is not possible at all).** The merge answers "does not merge" and immediately yields a conflict set (Г21) if **any** of the following holds: (а) the base, the local or the server version is not valid UTF-8, contains `0x00`, or exceeds the limit (Ж27); (б) it is not a JSON object, or a key is repeated in any object (`serde_json` silently takes the last one, so the parser must itself reject a repeat); (в) `version` is not 1 in at least one of the three versions; (г) `notes` is not an object, the value of any note is not an array, an array element is not an object, the known fields `id`, `name`, `icon`, `propertyType`, `value`, `settings` have a non-string value, `id` is empty; (д) an `id` is repeated in one list. This is the "garbage" from the owner's requirements: it is neither merged nor "repaired". A version that does not pass admission stays as it is in the set (`other`), the base (if there was one) in `base`.
- **Ж5. The merge units are keys, not lines.** The merge goes over the tree `version` → `notes` → `<amby-id>` → property list → property → field. Values are compared by JSON meaning, not by bytes (whitespace, dictionary key order, `é` and `é` are not distinguished), so "reformatting" the file does not count as an edit. Numbers and unknown values are compared by the number's text (with `arbitrary_precision`): `1.0` and `1` are different values, so as not to lose information.
- **Ж6. The top level.** `version` is the same in all three (Ж4 в). Unknown top-level fields (not `version`, not `notes`) are "top-level keys" with indivisible values: one changed by one side is taken; one deleted by one side and untouched by the other is deleted; one changed differently, or deleted by one side and changed by the other — a **conflict** of the place. All of them are kept in the result, ordered by name (Ж13).
- **Ж7. Notes (`notes`).** The key is the `amby-id` (ULID), not the path: renaming and moving a note do not touch the file and do not trigger a merge. An empty list and an absent key are **the same thing** (the app writes it that way: the empty key is deleted); the result contains no empty lists. For each `amby-id` from the union of the keys of the three versions (normalized per the previous sentence): if one side's list equals the base — the other side's list is taken; if both are equal — either; otherwise a list merge (Ж8). Keys of notes that are not on disk (a note in the trash, one that did not arrive, a deleted one) are **never deleted by the merge**: the engine does not know which notes exist, and deletes an entry only if one side explicitly deleted (emptied) the list and the other did not touch it.
- **Ж8. A note's property list.** A merge in three steps over the elements matched by the property's `id`.
  1. **Membership.** For each `id` from the union: present only on one side (not in the base) — added, taken; present in the base and gone on one side, unchanged on the other — deleted; gone on both — deleted; present on both — Ж9. Present in the base, **gone on one side and changed on the other** (any field, taking Ж9 into account) — a **conflict** "deletion versus edit". Added on both sides with the same `id` (does not happen with ULIDs, but is checked): if the elements are equal — one, otherwise a conflict.
  2. **Names.** After step 1 the property names of the note (ASCII case-insensitively, like `upsert`) must be unique. If two **different** `id`s gave one name: when their `icon`, `propertyType`, `value`, `settings` are equal — one remains, with the smaller `id`, the other is removed as coinciding (nothing is lost: the data is the same; the removed `id` goes into the report for the interface, Ж16); otherwise — a **conflict** "same name". This protects the `upsert` invariant (lookup by name) and the impossibility of identical keys in the YAML header.
  3. **Order.** The order is the sequence of `id`s of the surviving properties. A three-way line merge per Г12 is taken (diff3, `id` as "lines"): base → local, base → server, and inserted `id`s go to the places of their sides. With no conflict, the order of the result is unambiguous (Г12 item 5). Order is presentation, not data, so **there is no conflict over order alone**: if the order edits overlap (both sides reordered the same adjacent properties differently), the order of the side whose `id` sequence (of the survivors, before insertions) is lexicographically smaller (bytewise, by `id`) is taken; the `id`s inserted by either side are then placed by the diff3 rule above. The choice is deterministic and symmetric (Г15 г). The note goes into the report "order taken from one device" (Ж16), and the interface can say so. The order of properties untouched by the edits does not change.
- **Ж9. Property fields.** A property that is present in both versions and in the base is merged by **places**; a place is a set of fields that are edited together: (а) `name`; (б) `icon`; (в) `propertyType`, `settings` and `value` together (a value is meaningful only for its own type and set of options: an edit of `settings` by one side and of `value` by the other can give a value outside the options); (г) each unknown field is its own place. A place changed by one side is taken; changed by both identically — taken; changed differently — a **conflict** (place: note, property, field). An edit of `name` by one side and of `value` by the other merge (rename + value edit). Value strings are **atomic**: the text inside `value` is not merged.
- **Ж10. All or nothing (like Г12 item 6).** If there is even one place conflict, the resulting file is not written: the conflict set contains both versions and the base (Г21), and the list of conflict areas is stored and shown by the interface (Ж17). A partial merge (merge everything except the disputed notes) is not done — it would leave on disk a file that no device ever had. The merge is deterministic from three inputs and **symmetric**: `merge(b, l, r)` and `merge(b, r, l)` — both clean with the same bytes or both conflicts (Г15 г).
- **Ж11. Short paths and the order of checks.** As in Г13: the Ж4 admission (parsing all three); local and server are equal (by JSON meaning) — the result is local; local equals the base — server; server equals the base — local. "Equal" means equal **by bytes** for choosing the returned bytes: if a side equals the base by meaning but not by bytes (someone reformatted), the bytes of the **other side** are taken; if that one also equals by meaning — local. For these paths the bytes are taken unchanged: the merge does not rewrite them (Ж12). Then comes the three-way merge (Ж6–Ж9) and the Ж14 checks.

### Ж.3. Writing the result and stability

- **Ж12. What counts as untouched.** If the result matches one of the sides by meaning, that side's bytes are written as they are (Ж11). Only when the result is really composed of the edits of both sides is it **serialized anew** (Ж13). JSON has no user text that cannot be rewritten: dictionary keys and whitespace are format, not data, and **the app itself rebuilds the whole file on every write** (Ж.0). Acceptable normalization in the merge: key order, whitespace, escaping of characters in strings (except for values preserved byte-for-byte — there are none). Unacceptable: loss or change of any value, any property, any unknown field, a change of numbers or of the order of list elements.
- **Ж13. Canonical form.** The result of the merge, like all app writes after the Ж20 change, is written like this: 2-space indent, `"version"`, then `"notes"`; the `notes` keys ascending (bytewise by UTF-8); a property's fields go in the order `id`, `name`, `icon`, `propertyType`, `value`, `settings`, then unknown fields ascending by name; unknown top-level fields after `notes` ascending; one newline at the end. This is the same form the app writes; so the next app write after a merge does not change the file needlessly, and two devices that merged the same thing get **identical bytes** and link the record with no new conflict (Д15).
- **Ж14. BOM and line endings.** As Г14, but at file level: if the BOM is the same on both sides, it stays; otherwise the one that differs from the base is taken; if both differ from the base in different ways — no BOM. Line endings: both `\n` or both `\r\n` — that one; otherwise what differs from the base is taken; with two different ones — `\n`. The Ж13 result is written with this BOM and line ending. This is only the form of the file.
- **Ж15. Checks of the result.** The merge answers with a conflict, not a clean result, if the result (а) does not pass the Ж4 admission (an internal check: the merged file is parsed back into the same tree); (б) lost a property that was in any of the versions and was not explicitly deleted per Ж8 (the "no value is lost" check as a defense against an error in the merge itself: the multiset of pairs `(amby-id, property id, field, value)` of the result contains everything the rules Ж8–Ж9 must preserve); (в) the Ж21 protection is violated. A conflict for these reasons is a set, as usual.
- **Ж16. Write, report, events.** As Г16–Г18: a clean result is written by `StagedWrite::publish(Some(SyncOverwrite))`, the previous content stays as a snapshot (Д53); in the state the record gets the server version, the result goes out at the next step Д22; `CycleReport.merged_paths` contains `.amby/properties.json`. A repeat after a crash gives the same result (Г18). After the file is written the app (not the engine) must re-read the properties: call `restore_cache` for SQLite and tell the interface that the properties of open notes have changed (Ж20 в).

### Ж.4. A real conflict and how it is resolved

- **Ж17. The conflict set is the same, the list of areas is new.** A conflict under Ж10 is a set under Г21 (`bothEdited`, `other.json`, `base.json`, `meta.json` with no format change: `v` stays 1). The set format **does not change**; the interface knows the file is `.amby/properties.json` from `path` in `meta.json` (Г27). The set is synchronized (Г24), so it is visible and resolvable **from any device**: the problem of "the second copy stays on one device" went away already with К2; Ж removes the need for this decision in most cases.
- **Ж18. The port for such a set.** In `Г32`, for a `.amby/properties.json` set the `text` field is `true` (contrary to Г28) **only if** `current` and `other` pass the Ж4 admission check; otherwise `text = false` and resolution is `Current`/`Other` as a whole. For `text = true` the port adds **`propertiesView`**: a list of areas `{noteId, notePath|null, noteTitle|null, propertyId, propertyName, kind: "edit"|"deleteVsEdit"|"sameName"|"order"|"top", base, current, other}` for the conflicting places, and also `autoMerged` — how many places were merged automatically. The app takes the note's title and path from the index by `amby-id` (not in the index — `null`, and the `amby-id` is shown). The file is **not** passed through IPC in full.
- **Ж19. Resolution.** Three ways: `Current`, `Other` as a whole (Г33), and the new `{kind:"properties", picks:[{place, side:"current"|"other"}]}`: for each area one of the variants is chosen; all other places are taken from the clean merge (Ж8–Ж9); the result is serialized (Ж13) and written as `Assembled` (an ordinary write with a snapshot, Г26). The choice must cover **all** areas; otherwise the error is `incomplete_picks` and nothing changes. "Deletion versus edit": `current`/`other` chooses whether the property stays (the edited one) or is deleted. "Same name": the choice keeps one of the properties; keeping **both** is not possible (the name invariant) — the second is offered for renaming (the app renames it to `Имя (2)` (Name (2)) as a separate action after the choice, not in the merge). If any version changed while the person was choosing — "A new version arrived while you were comparing" (К4): nothing is written. After resolution, on other devices the set and the file arrive the usual way (Г26).
- **Ж20. Changes in the app.** (a) `property_store::write` writes in the canonical form of Ж13: `BTreeMap` instead of `HashMap`; (b) extra fields are **preserved** on read-write (`#[serde(flatten)]` into `extra`, for both the file and the property) instead of being silently dropped: otherwise the merge would keep a foreign field and the first edit in the app would erase it; (c) after a file is written by sync (merge, resolution, an ordinary arrival of a version), `restore_cache` is called and an event is sent to the interface (Ж16); (d) resolution with `picks` and `propertiesView` (Ж18–Ж19). The canonical rewrite (Ж13) happens **only on a real edit in the app**, not in a background normalization pass: an app update does not create a wave of versions that differ only in form. The format version stays 1: canonical order and preservation of extra fields do not change the meaning of the file, and previous app versions read such a file as before. The test `malformed_sidecar_is_not_overwritten` and the behavior "an unreadable file is not overwritten" are preserved.
- **Ж21. Protection against mass loss of values.** The merge is already counted as a single file replacement in Д54 (Г3: there is one file). That is not enough: a file with thousands of properties is still one file. Therefore a merge (not an ordinary replacement by one side) answers with a **conflict** if the result, relative to the base, deletes **20 or more** property entries **and** at least 10% of the base's entries (thresholds as in Д54). This way an accidental emptying of properties on one device that coincided with someone else's edit does not go through silently. A one-sided replacement (Д30, Д33) is protected like every file: by a history snapshot (Д53) and by keeping the version on the server for 30 days. See question Ж.9-4.

### Ж.5. Interaction with notes

- **Ж22. The note header.** Properties live in two places: the `properties.json` file (the data of Ж.0) and the note's YAML header (a mirror and the user's own keys). The header is merged by Г11 separately, the properties by Ж8–Ж9; the engine **does not know about the link** and does not bring one into line with the other. Possible divergences after merges (and the example "renamed on one device, edited the value on the other": the file merges, the header has a key conflict) are acceptable: the data is intact; conflicts are shown separately. In the `properties.json` conflict card (Ж18), the interface shows alongside, if the same note (by `amby-id`) has a conflict set (Г25), the link "This note also has a text conflict". No automatic reconciliation of the header with properties is introduced by this appendix (question Ж.9-5).
- **Ж23. Renames, moves, deletion and restoration of notes.** The key is `amby-id`, not the path: renaming and moving change nothing. Deleting a note (to the trash, Д52) does not touch the properties file: the entries stay, as now (neither `delete_note` nor sync removes them), so restoring from the trash brings back the properties too; the merge does not remove them either (Ж7). A copy of a note with the same `amby-id` (blocked by the identity guard) is not covered by the properties file.
- **Ж24. Properties moved into a database.** `backup_and_clear` removes the note's entry from the file and writes an immutable backup to `property-backups/` (synchronized as a new file with no conflicts). If the same properties were being edited on another device at that time, the merge gives "deletion versus edit" (Ж8) — a **conflict**, not "the edit wins" (as in Р7 for files): silently resurrecting the property would create a second source of data next to the database. The backup stays untouched.
- **Ж25. Changing `amby-id`.** The merge does not rename the `notes` keys; changing a note's `amby-id` (manually in the header) is a separate operation outside sync (Г13 a forbids merging the header on a change). An orphaned key remains as data.
- **Ж26. One of the sides does not parse.** Merge: the Ж4 admission check, a conflict set, **no attempts to repair**. A one-sided replacement (Д30, Д33) for `.amby/properties.json` is also checked: if the version about to be written to disk does not pass Ж4 while the local file does, it is **not written**: a conflict set is created, where `other` is the unreadable version, and **the readable local file stays at the path**. This is a deliberate departure from Г2 ("the server version is at the path"): the app does not read garbage and would show empty properties, while nothing is lost — both versions are intact, the unreadable one lies in the set and is visible on all devices (Г24). If the local one does not pass either — the ordinary rule, as for any file. The record state stays on the previous server version, so the next pass does not produce more sets (the set name is computed from the content, Г22). Resolution is as in Ж19 (`Other` writes the unreadable version only on an explicit choice). Tests: Ж.8.

### Ж.6. Limits and fallback paths

- **Ж27. Limits.** `PROPS_MERGE_MAX_BYTES` = 16,000,000 bytes (decimal) for each of the three versions; no more than 200,000 property entries in total in a version; no more than 1,000 in one note's list; depth is fixed by the schema, and unknown values nest no deeper than 32 (parsing with a depth limit). Exceeding a limit means "does not merge" (Ж4), a conflict set, no loss. Time: parsing is linear; order merging follows Г12 item 7 (the product of the number of `id`s in a note's list, at most 25,000,000 — at the limit of 1,000 this is 1,000,000, so the limit is unreachable). Memory is on the order of three parse trees; at 16 MB that is on the order of a hundred MB. The real size is measured separately (a bench note in the tests, does not block the build).
- **Ж28. There is always exactly one fallback path.** Any reason for "does not merge" or "too complex" (Ж4, Ж15, Ж21, Ж27, base not found under Г10) leads to a conflict set. The file at the path stays the server version, the local one goes into `other` (Г2); nothing is lost. The merge never blocks a pass and never throws an error because of the file's content.

### Ж.7. What changes in earlier decisions, and migration

| Was                                                                                         | Now                                                                                               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Г5: "JSON, `properties.json` are never merged automatically (Р7)"                           | the same, **except** `.amby/properties.json` (Ж1); other JSON and database manifests — as before  |
| Г6, Г8: a base only for `.md`                                                               | and for `.amby/properties.json` (Ж3)                                                              |
| Г4: merge in Д29/Д31                                                                        | the same, one place of action; for `properties.json` — the function of Ж5–Ж15                     |
| Г28: a file not under Г5 — `text = false`                                                   | `.amby/properties.json` — `text = true` with admissible versions (Ж18), resolution by areas (Ж19) |
| Р7: "`properties.json` and manifests… a list of conflicts… "keep mine" or "keep the other"" | `properties.json` is merged by keys; manifests — as before                                        |
| Э5: "merging `properties.json` conflicts by keys" (moved from Э4)                           | done earlier, before the final gates (Ж.8)                                                        |
| Section 8: "frequent conflicts in `properties.json` — merge by keys if needed (Р7)"         | merge by keys (Ж)                                                                                 |
| App write: `HashMap`, extra fields dropped                                                  | canonical form, extra fields preserved (Ж20)                                                      |

- **Ж29. Bases for files synchronized before the update — one rule for all mergeable paths.** Existing entries have no base, and Г8 updates it only on events. On the first pass after the update, for every entry of a mergeable path (`.md` under Г5 and `.amby/properties.json`) whose local file by hash equals the one recorded in the state, and whose size is within its limit (Г5 / Ж27), the engine writes the base once from the file's bytes (`base_put`, then as in Г9). If the file is already changed, there is no base, and the first divergence is a conflict set (Г10). This is an addition to Г8 for notes as well; tests for `.md` are mandatory (Ж.8).
- **Ж30. Former local copies** (kept after the Opus review: cheap, and it protects those who have them)**.** Versions before К2 put a second version `.amby/properties.<12 hex>-conflict.json` next to the file; they were not synchronized (Р4). If such files exist, with sync on the app once turns each **admissible** (Ж4) file into a `bothEdited` conflict set without a base: `other` is the bytes of the copy, the `id` formula of Г22 with the number of the entry's server version at that moment and the hash of the copy; the copy goes to the vault trash (Г26 item 2) **after** the set is recognized as existing (Г23); repeating is harmless. An inadmissible copy (damaged) the app does not touch and shows as before (Г29). Parsing goes through the shared admission code of Ж4. The rule is needed to preserve the former copies for those who have them.
- **Ж31. Old and new client.** The file on the server is still plain JSON of version 1: a client without Ж reads it as before (Ж20). The conflict set is the same format of Г21. There are no new protocol fields; there is no need to change the format or protocol version.

### Ж.8. Order and tests

- **Order.** Ж does not depend on the code of appendix Е (limits and plans). Implementation goes after Е is merged into `dev` (so as not to conflict in `apply.rs`/`decide.rs`), before the final run of the gates (mutations and simulation must already test the `properties.json` merge). This record → Opus review → a red-tests session → a code session.
- **Engine (core, `sync/core/tests/engine_props_merge*.rs`).** A table of the cases of Ж5–Ж11: edits to different notes, to different properties of one note, to different fields of one property; identical edits; an edit against an edit of the same place; deletion versus edit; both deleted; addition on both sides (different `id`s, the same name — equal and different data); order: one side, both differently (deterministic choice, symmetry, a note in the report), a permutation plus an insertion; an empty list and a missing key; unknown fields (top level, note, property) are preserved and conflict under Ж6, Ж9; the Ж4 admission check: each reason, for each of the three versions; a repeated key; not a string; numbers and escaping; the limit on size, number of entries, depth; BOM and line endings (Ж14); canonical form and idempotence (`serialize(parse(x))` is stable, the two orders of sides give identical bytes); short paths return the bytes unchanged (Ж11); the Ж21 guard on both sides of the thresholds.
- **Properties (`engine_props_merge_props.rs`, random trees).** (a) `merge(x, x, x) = x`. (b) `merge(b, a, b) = a`, `merge(b, b, a) = a`. (c) `merge(b, a, a) = a`. (d) symmetry of `merge(b, l, r)` ↔ `merge(b, r, l)`. (e) **no value is lost:** in a clean result, every value of any field of any property that was added or changed by either side is present, unless it was replaced by an edit of the same cell by the other side or an explicit deletion; every unknown field is present. (f) the result is an admissible Ж4 file, version 1, names unique. (g) the result matches what the app writes (Ж13) after reading.
- **Ж26 and Ж29.** Ж26: an unreadable server version with a readable local file — a set, the path keeps the local one; both unreadable — an ordinary replacement; repeating the pass does not produce more sets; `Other` writes the version only on an explicit choice. Ж29: tests the same for `.md` and `properties.json`.
- **Base and state.** `engine_base_store.rs` is extended with the `.amby/properties.json` path; Ж29 (a one-time base, an already changed file, an unequal hash, a crash between writing the base and the state).
- **Two-device scenarios (`merge_props.rs`, `session_tests`).** Edits to different notes merge without a conflict and without a set; a conflict on one property — the set is visible on the second device; resolution by areas clears the set on both; a damaged side — a set; deletion versus edit; a failure at each call (a repeat gives the same bytes); the Д54 guard counts a merge as one replacement; `merged_paths` contains the path; a `sync-overwrite` snapshot is taken.
- **Simulation (`sync/sim`).** New invariants: "edits to properties of different notes or different properties converge without a conflict and without loss of values"; "a real property conflict leaves both versions in the set and both values reachable"; "**no property value is lost** under any order of events, failures and repeats" (checked against the set of all values ever written by the devices, versus the result plus the contents of the sets); "after convergence all devices have identical `properties.json` bytes"; gates: the properties file is included in the mutation targets (the `apply.rs` merge, the new property-merge module, the `decide` branches with a base) and in the long simulation.
- **App (Rust).** `property_store`: the canonical form and key order are deterministic; extra fields are preserved on `upsert`/`delete`/`reorder`; a damaged file and one of unknown version are not overwritten (the earlier tests); after a write by sync, `restore_cache` updates the table (Ж16); `propertiesView` (areas, notes not in the index, the size limit); resolution with `picks` (a full and an incomplete choice, a stale version); the Ж30 copy migration (admissible, damaged, repeat, a crash between the set and the trash); `rules_doc_tests` keeps the `vault-format.md` table consistent with the engine.
- **Interface (vitest) and browser.** The property conflict card: areas, the "mine"/"other" labels by `createdHere` (Г34), a choice for each area, "Done" is blocked until all are chosen, the stale-version banner, a link to the text conflict of the same note (Ж22), translations in all languages in `src/locales/resources.ts`. A native run of two windows/devices — per `docs/native-ui-smoke.md`.

### Ж.9. Opus decisions on the open questions (24 October)

1. **A damaged incoming file:** a check is needed before the gates; the unreadable version goes into a set, and the readable local file stays at the path (Ж26, the departure from Г2 is explained there).
2. **Deletion versus edit:** the conflict is accepted (the owner delegated technical data-safety decisions).
3. **`value` + `settings` + `propertyType` as one place:** accepted.
4. **The Ж21 threshold:** accepted. **Order:** there is no conflict over order alone, the choice is deterministic and symmetric, a note in the report (Ж8 item 3).
5. **Header and properties:** there is no automatic reconciliation in Ж; a separate stage later, based on data.
6. **Limit:** 16,000,000 bytes (Ж27); the measurement stays a note-test.
7. **Bases for files from before К1:** generalized to all mergeable paths (Ж29).
8. **Other `.amby/` files:** stay conflicts until there is data (Ж2).
9. **Copy migration:** kept (Ж30).

## Appendix З. Note properties: values in the header, presentation by groups

The owner's decision of 4 November 2026. The appendix **replaces appendix Ж** (merging a single `properties.json`) and changes the property storage format. Written before the code (section 6): this record → Opus review → a separate red-tests session → a separate code session with the tests frozen. Items `З…`, open questions — З.7.

### З.0. Why

- **Two truths.** Right now a property's value lies both in `.amby/properties.json` (`value`) and in the note's header (`Name: value`). The app shows the value from the file, so an edit to the header in Obsidian or by hand diverges from it.
- **One file for the whole vault.** Any edits to the properties of two different notes on two computers are a conflict of the whole file (the card "Note properties were changed on two computers").
- **Scale.** With 1,000,000 notes, if a third have presentation, about 200 bytes per note, the file takes ~60 MB: every icon edit rewrites, encrypts and sends it whole.
- **What we keep.** Type, icon, settings and order stay **each note's own** (the owner rejected a shared schema per property name, as in Obsidian). The note's header stays clean: only `Name: value`, no Amby service keys in it. No more than 256 files regardless of vault size.

### З.1. Where things live

- **З1. The value is only in the note's header.** The only source of a property's value is a key of the note's YAML header. The property files have no `value` field. The value is read by property name: first an exact key match, otherwise the single match ignoring ASCII case; several matches — the property is shown empty with a warning, nothing is written. Writing a value is as now, `upsert_frontmatter_property` (lossless, BOM and line endings preserved, a history snapshot before the write).
- **З2. Presentation is only in the group file.** (The form of an entry is З47: only differences from the template, without the property's `id`.) For each note: an ordered list of properties `{id, name, icon, propertyType, settings}`. The list order is the order in the panel. The name is unique within a note ignoring ASCII case (as now). Unknown fields of the file, the note and the property are **preserved** on read and write (`#[serde(flatten)] extra`), so that a newer version of Amby does not lose its data on an older one.
- **З3. A YAML key without presentation** is an ordinary header property, as now for foreign keys (header lines in the panel). **Presentation without a YAML key** ("dormant") is shown in the note's properties panel **below the active ones, in gray**, with the label "not in the header" (нет в шапке) and two actions: «Вернуть» (Restore) (the key is added to the header with an empty value, the property is active again) and «Удалить окончательно» (Delete permanently) (the entry is removed from the group; the previous group file stays as a snapshot in the history, Д53). The entry does not delete itself and applies again when a key with that name appears (deleted the key in Obsidian and brought it back — the presentation is intact). The owner's decision (З.7-1).
- **З3а. A key renamed outside Amby.** If a note has a dormant entry and a new YAML key without presentation, and the new key's value matches the value the dormant property had in the last known form of the header (the index remembers the previous header), the properties panel shows a hint: "It looks like «Статус» (Status) was renamed to «Состояние» (State). Move the type and icon to it?" Agreeing renames the presentation entry (one entry per group, the header is not touched); "No" hides the hint for this name pair on this note on all devices: the pair is recorded in the note's entry in the group (`dismissedRenames: [["Статус", "Состояние"]]`, preserved as an unknown field by older versions, merged together with the note's entry under З16); the record is removed when one of the two names ceases to exist. Without a value match there is no hint: both properties are visible as they are (the new one as ordinary, the old one in gray under З3). The owner's decision (З.7-2).
- **З4. An empty note is not stored.** A note without presentation entries is absent from the group; a group without notes has no file. The exception is a tombstone with a `legacy` mark (З36).

### З.2. Group format

> **The format is replaced by section З.11 (4 November 2026, the owner's decision):** 16 groups `prop_<0–f>.jsonl`, one line — one note, a template of base properties, only differences in an entry. Items З5–З8 below are history; З45–З56 are in force.

- **З5. Path.** `.amby/properties/<g>.json`, where `<g>` is the two lowercase hexadecimal digits of the first byte of BLAKE3 of the `amby-id` string (UTF-8). Not the start of the ULID: the first characters of a ULID are the creation time, so notes of one period would gather in one group. 256 groups; with 1,000,000 notes a group is ~230 KB.
- **З6. Layout.** `.amby/properties/layout.json`: `{"version": 1, "groups": 256, "hash": "blake3-first-byte"}`. Read before the groups; a different number of groups or an unknown version — properties are read-only, nothing is written, the warning "The properties format is newer than this version of Amby". Changing the number of groups in the future is a separate versioned migration, never a split on the fly.
- **З7. The group file.**

  ```json
  {
    "version": 1,
    "notes": {
      "<amby-id>": {
        "hint": { "path": "Папка/Заметка.md", "title": "Заметка" },
        "properties": [
          {
            "id": "<ULID>",
            "name": "Статус",
            "icon": "📌",
            "propertyType": "select",
            "settings": "Идея,Готово"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }
  ```

  `hint` is a hint for a human and for re-linking (З11), **not a key**; it is updated only when the app is writing this note's presentation anyway, not on file rename (otherwise every rename would change the group).

- **З8. Canonical form.** `notes` keys ascending, fields in structure order, a 2-space indent, a newline at the end; the BOM and line endings of the previous file are preserved (`frontmatter::atomic_write`). Writing happens only on a real edit, with no background normalization.

### З.3. Edits in the app

- **З9. Order of writing the two files.** An edit that touches both the header and the presentation (renaming a property, a type change that changes the value record, creating a property) goes like this: (1) the group is written with a `previousName` field on the property; (2) the note's header (the key is renamed or the value is rewritten); (3) `previousName` is removed. Reading matches a key to `name` **or** `previousName`, so a failure between the steps loses nothing and does not produce dormant entries; the next launch completes step 3 (refined by З28, З29: check before writing, a local journal, only the device that started completes it; the order of creation and deletion). An edit of presentation only (icon, settings, order) writes one group; an edit of the value only — one note.
- **З10. Vault check.** Shows: dormant entries (З3), entries without a note (З11), ambiguous keys (З1). Actions: link to a note, delete to the vault trash (`.amby/trash/`, restorable). Nothing is deleted silently.

### З.4. Note identity

- **З11. `amby-id` changed.** The presentation entry under the old ID stays (it is never deleted automatically). If the index sees that the file at the same path has a different ID and the old ID occurs nowhere else, the app offers: "The identifier of the note «…» has changed. Link its former properties to it?". Agreeing moves the entry (possibly to another group: two writes, first the new one, then deleting the old one). Without agreement nothing changes.
- **З12. Duplicate IDs** — as now: property edits are blocked until the ID becomes unique.
- **З13. Deleting a note.** ~~While the note is in the vault trash, the entry stays (restoring brings the properties back). The entry is deleted when the note is permanently deleted from the trash.~~ **Replaced by З34:** deleting a note never removes the entry.

### З.5. Synchronization

- **З14. What is synchronized.** `.amby/properties/` (all groups and `layout.json`) is added to the synchronized list (`sync/core/src/engine/rules.rs`). `.amby/properties.json` stays synchronized for the sake of older versions (З18).
- **З15. Base.** Groups get a base on the same terms as notes (Ж3, Ж29).
- **З16. Merge by notes.** Three-way, the unit is **the whole note entry** (`notes.<amby-id>`), by the meaning of the JSON, not by bytes: the entry changed on one side — it is taken; on both the same way — it; on both differently — a conflict of this note. Deletion of an entry versus its edit — a conflict of this note. Unknown top-level fields — as one entry. No merging inside a note (by properties) is done; if needed it is added later without changing the format.
- **З17. Conflict and its resolution.** All or nothing per file (as Г12 item 6): if even one note conflicts, the group is not written and a conflict set is created (Г21). The port returns a list of conflicting notes `{noteId, notePath|null, noteTitle|null}` and how many entries were merged automatically. Resolution: for **each** conflicting note choose "from this computer" or "from the other one"; the rest is taken from the clean merge; the result is written as `Assembled` with a snapshot. An incomplete choice is an error, nothing changes. The card: "The properties of the note «Идея» (Idea) were changed on two computers" (one line per note). Preserved: protection against mass loss (Ж21, thresholds by note entries; refined by З32 — counted over the whole pass), an unreadable side (Ж26), the single fallback path — a conflict set (Ж28).
- **З18. Values merge with notes.** Values live in the header, so they are merged by header keys (Г11) together with the note's text; there is no separate mechanism.

### З.6. Migration from `properties.json` version 1

- **З19. A preliminary check without writing.** A report: how many notes and properties; where the value in the file matches the header (expected almost everywhere); where it diverges; where there is no key in the header; notes with a damaged header, without an ID or with a duplicate ID; entries without a note.
- **З20. Decisions in the report.** It matches — `value` is simply not carried over. There is no key in the header and the value is not empty — the value is **written to the header** (this is an edit of the note, shown in the report as a list). It diverges — by default the header's value stays, and in the report for each one the value from the file can be chosen. A damaged header, no ID, a duplicate — the note is not touched, its presentation is carried over, the value stays in the report until it is fixed. Entries without a note are carried over as they are (З11).
- **З21. Order.** Confirmation by the vault owner → backups (`properties.json` as a whole and each note whose header we edit, bytes as they are) → the `.amby/migrations/` journal → writing the groups → header edits → `layout.json` last (the completion marker) → writing the outcome. A repeat after a failure completes it per the journal. Rollback: delete the groups and `layout.json`, restore the notes from the backups, `properties.json` was not touched.
- **З22. The old file.** `properties.json` after migration is **neither deleted nor changed**: older versions of Amby on other devices keep reading it. The new version does not read it. If after migration it changed (an older version on another device edited it), the new version **itself carries over** the changed note entries by the same path as З19–З21 (~~comparison with the copy of the file at the time of migration~~ — by the `legacy` marks in the entries, З36; backup and journal — as in the migration), and shows the hint "Properties changed by an older version of Amby on another device have been carried over: N notes". If the same note was also changed here after migration, the auto-carry-over does not touch it: it goes into the hint as a conflict with the choice "keep the local one" / "take from the old version" (З.7-3). On the sync screen — a warning while one of the account's devices has an older version (by the client version in the device list, if the server knows it; otherwise by the fact of a `properties.json` change).
- **З23. Cache.** The `note_custom_properties` table in SQLite is built from the groups and headers; `value` is taken from the header. Rebuilding the index migrates and deletes nothing.

### З.7. Questions for the owner

1. ~~Dormant presentation~~ — **decided 04.11:** below the active ones, greyed out, with «Вернуть» (Restore) and «Удалить окончательно» (Delete permanently) (З3).
2. ~~Renaming a key outside Amby~~ — **decided 04.11:** a hint in the properties panel (З3а).
3. ~~Old versions on other devices~~ — **decided 04.11:** hints with automatic carry-over are enough; the new version does not write to `properties.json` (З22).

### З.8а. Plan review

The plan was written on 4 November 2026 (Opus 5.5) together with the owner: storage options, scale, identity, old versions. The same day it was reviewed by a fresh Opus 5.5 session against section 6; the corrections are in З.9 (items З24–З44), the questions for the owner are in З.10 (both decided 04.11). The owner's decisions (values only in the header, each note has its own presentation, 256 groups, no per-note file, no encoding in the key name, no shared per-name schema) were not changed. Next is the red-tests session per З.8.

### З.8. Tests (written before the code)

Each test carries the requirement number in a comment (`// З31: …`). Listed by area:

- **Format (`property_store`, Rust).** Group path (BLAKE3 of the first byte of `amby-id`, two lowercase hex digits, 256 groups, distribution); `layout.json` and refusal to write on a foreign format (З6); preserving unknown fields of the file, the entry and the property (З2); canonical form and idempotence (З8, З30); an empty entry is not stored, a tombstone is stored (З4, З36).
