- **Matching (З1, З24–З27).** Exact, ASCII case-insensitive, NFC, multiple matches; non-string keys and `amby-*` are not matched, a name `amby-…` is rejected; writing into the found spelling of the key; a corrupted header, no ID, duplicate ID — no dormant entries, no hints, edits rejected, nothing written; a complex value is read-only; a type change with loss does not rewrite the value; a database page — the database's keys are not touched, `backup_and_clear` on groups.
- **Dormant entries and hints (З3, З3а, З44).** «Вернуть» (Restore), «Удалить окончательно» (Delete permanently) (a copy in `property-backups/` before deletion); the hint only for a non-empty value and a single pair; a dismissal is recorded in `dismissedRenames`, cleared only by a real write; the old-version warning and the migration report contain the З37 phrase.
- **Two-file write (З9, З28, З29).** A failure at each step for rename, create, delete, type change (via `inject_nth_atomic_write_failure`); a preliminary refusal with no write; completion from the log on the device that started it; another device with `previousName` only reads; the keys `name` and `previousName` at the same time.
- **Identity and deletion (З11, З12, З34, З35).** Deleting a note (all `files.deleteMode` modes) does not touch the entry; restoring brings the properties back; re-binding to another group, a failure between steps, a retry, the new ID already has a different entry.
- **Engine (`sync/core/tests/engine_props_groups*.rs`).** Per-entry merge (one side, both identically, both differently, deletion vs. edit, added by both — equal and different); `hint` and `dismissedRenames` do not produce a conflict, the choice is symmetric (З31); the З30 tolerance — each reason on each of the three versions; `layout.json` is not merged; short paths return the bytes as is; BOM and line endings; symmetry and repeatability; the per-pass protection З32 — both sides of the threshold, a sum over many groups with 1–2 entries, one-sided replacement, incoming and outgoing; the unreadable side З33; the base for groups (З15, Ж29); `rules.rs` syncs `.amby/properties/` but not `.amby/property-ops/`. Properties on random trees — as in Ж.8 (`merge(x,x,x)=x`, symmetry, no entry is lost).
- **Resolution (З17).** A choice for each note, an incomplete choice is an error with no write, a stale version — nothing is written.
- **Migration (З19–З21, З38–З42).** A report without a single entry written to disk; each З20 decision; "matches" by YAML meaning (list, flag, number, empty); a failure at each step and a retry from the log; a note edited between the failure and the retry; rollback before and after `layout.json` (a header edited after the migration is not restored); `properties.json` byte-for-byte unchanged; an unreadable `properties.json`; two devices migrated the same vault — identical bytes; there is no migration if `layout.json` came from the server.
- **Old versions (З22, З36).** Carrying over an edit from the old version; a repeated check does not carry the same difference a second time; an edit here and there — a conflict hint, both choices; a property deleted by the old version; what was deleted here does not come back to life; a note added by the old version; values from `properties.json` are not carried over.
- **Scale (a benchmark test, does not block the build, З43).** 1,000,000 notes: time of a single edit, group size, cache rebuild time, migration report time.

### З.9. Review decisions (4 November 2026, Opus 5.5)

The owner delegated the technical data-safety decisions to the review; what the user sees and what is debatable are the questions in З.10. Items that have been refined are marked with a link in place.

**Matching a key to a property (З1)**

- **З24. Which keys are compared.** Only keys that YAML parses as a string (numbers and `true` as keys are never matched — they are ordinary header strings). Names are compared after normalizing to NFC (the header bytes do not change): a header written on macOS in NFD would otherwise produce a "dormant" entry. The order is З1: exact, then a single ASCII case-insensitive match. `amby-*` keys (`is_reserved_system_key`) are not matched; a property name starting with `amby-` is rejected on creation and rename, and one that came from the migration is shown in the З10 review. If the match was found case-insensitively, the value is written **into the found key with its spelling**: the current `upsert` would write by the entry's name and create a second key. Several matches — as in З1, and the entry is not considered dormant.
- **З25. The header cannot be read.** The header is corrupted, not closed, the root is not a mapping, there is a duplicate key; the note has no ID or it is duplicated (З12); the file was not read. Then the values are unavailable, **no entry is considered dormant**, there is no «Вернуть» (Restore), no «Удалить окончательно» (Delete permanently), no hints З3а and З11, and all property edits of the note are rejected (like `ensure_frontmatter_properties_available` does now). Otherwise a single typo in the header would show all the presentation greyed out and offer to delete it.
- **З26. Values that cannot be rewritten without loss.** A multi-line value, an anchor, a block string (`YamlValueKind::Complex`) is shown read-only ("edit in the source text"); editing the value and renaming such a key are rejected before the first write. A type change rewrites the value in the header only if the conversion `property_value_to_yaml_value` is reversible; otherwise (text "abc" → flag) the value stays as is and is shown as "does not fit the type".
- **З27. Databases.** The header keys of a database page belong to the database: its own resolver reads them (`resolve_property_yaml_value`), and the З rules do not change it. If a database page still has a presentation entry (an old version did not finish `backup_and_clear`), it does not apply to the database keys and is visible in the З10 review. `backup_and_clear` is rewritten for groups: a copy in `property-backups/` (as now) → removal of the entry from the group. З does not touch database files (`.ambd/`, manifests).

**Two-file write (З9)**

- **З28. Before the first write.** Each edit that touches both the header and the group: (a) checks that the header can be changed without loss (З25, З26, the key is unique), otherwise it refuses and nothing is written; (b) writes a local log `.amby/property-ops/<ULID>.json` (not synced, Р4; add to the table in `vault-format.md`): the note, the operation, the header hash before. The order per operation: **rename** — З9 (`previousName`); **create** — first the key in the header, then the entry (a failure leaves an ordinary key without presentation, not a dormant entry); **property deletion** — first the key from the header, then the entry (a failure leaves a visible dormant entry); **type change** — first the group, then the value (if it is rewritten per З26).
- **З29. Who completes after a failure.** Only the device that started the operation, from its own log, when the vault is opened: the header has the key `name` — remove `previousName`; there is only `previousName` — step 2 was not done, roll back step 1 (`name := previousName`); the header changed in another way — leave it as is and show it in the З10 review. Another device that received the group with `previousName` through sync only reads and completes nothing: the header may not have reached it yet. Reading by `previousName` happens only if there is no `name` key in the header. There is no full walk of the groups at startup.

**Group merge (З16, З17)**

- **З30. Tolerance.** Modeled on Ж4: UTF-8 without `0x00`; no more than `PROPS_MERGE_MAX_BYTES` = 16,000,000 bytes; a JSON object without duplicate keys at any level; `version` = 1 in all three versions; `notes` is an object; an entry is an object; `properties` is an array of objects, known fields are strings, `id` is non-empty and unique within the entry, names are unique within the entry ASCII case-insensitively; nesting of unknown values is no more than 32. If it fails — a conflict set (Ж28), no repair. Only the paths `.amby/properties/<two lowercase hex digits>.json` are merged; `layout.json` and other files in the folder are synced but not merged. The result is the canonical form З8; short paths return the side's bytes unchanged (as in Ж11); BOM and line endings — Ж14; symmetry — Ж10; repeatability — Г18.
- **З31. What is compared in an entry.** `hint` and `dismissedRenames` do not enter the conflict decision. `hint`: changed by one side — that side's; by both differently — the one whose canonical JSON is byte-wise smaller (deterministic and symmetric). `dismissedRenames` is a set of pairs, merged three-way as a set, no conflict is possible: otherwise dismissing a hint on one device and changing an icon on another would produce a conflict card. An entry added by both sides (absent in the base): equal without `hint` — one; otherwise a note conflict. The `legacy` mark (З36) is compared together with the properties.
- **З32. Protection against mass loss — per pass, not per file (refines Ж21 in З17).** With 300 notes with presentation, a group gets one or two entries, and the threshold "20 entries in a file" would never fire; and when one device erased everything and the other touched nothing, that is not a merge but a one-sided replacement of 256 files, which Д54 on a large vault would let through. The rule: after loading the group bodies and before writing them, the engine counts across **all** groups of the pass — merged and replaced one-sidedly, incoming and outgoing — how many `properties[]` elements disappear. If it is at least 20 and at least 10% of all elements in all groups on the device (the Д54 thresholds), no group of the pass is written or sent; the pass stops and asks, as in Д54: "Another device is deleting properties from N notes. Continue?" (owner's decision, З.10-1). The local groups stay on the paths.
- **З33. The unreadable side.** Ж26 for groups: the incoming group does not pass З30 while the local one does — it is not written to the path; a conflict set, the local one on the path.

**Deletion and identity**

- **З34. Deleting a note does not remove the entry (replaces З13).** By default a note goes to the system trash (`files.deleteMode = system`), and Amby does not learn when it was deleted permanently; each device has its own vault trash, and emptying it on one would erase the presentation of a note that another has already restored. Restoring from any trash brings the properties back: the entry is in place. An entry without a note is visible in the З10 review (bind, delete). Deleting an entry from the review and «Удалить окончательно» (Delete permanently) (З3) first write a copy of the entry to `property-backups/<note-id>/<ULID>.json` (like `backup_and_clear`), then remove the entry.
- **З35. Re-binding (refines З11).** Offered only if the new ID has no entry or it equals the old one (without `hint`); otherwise only the З10 review. The order: the new entry → a copy of the old one in `property-backups/` → deletion of the old one. A retry after a failure: the new one already equals the old one — only delete the old one. If agreed on two devices, identical entries merge without a conflict (З31); deleting the old one against its edit on another device is a note conflict (З16).

**Old versions side by side (З22)**

- **З36. Marks instead of a file copy.** Comparing "with a copy of the file at the moment of migration" loses data: after the first automatic carry-over the same difference would be carried over again, on top of newer local edits; the second device has no such copy at all. Instead of a copy, the note's entry stores a mark `legacy: {entry, record}`: the BLAKE3 of this note's canonical entry from `properties.json` (without `value`) and the BLAKE3 of the `properties` list right after the carry-over. The mark is synced together with the entry. The check runs only when the hash of `properties.json` differs from the one remembered on this device (`.amby/migrations/properties/legacy-state.json`, local; **not** in the root of `.amby/migrations/`: every `*.json` in the root is read as an ID migration log, and a foreign file there blocks reading notes — found by a manual check on 05.11). For each note from `properties.json` and each entry with a mark: the hash of the old entry equals `entry` — nothing; otherwise, if the current list equals `record` (not changed here) — carry-over (the list is taken from the old entry without `value`; no old entry — the list is empty); otherwise — the З22 conflict hint with two choices. After a carry-over or a choice the mark is updated. An entry with an empty list and a mark is stored (a tombstone, З4): otherwise a property deleted here would come back to life from `properties.json`. No mark but an old entry exists — the old version added it after the migration: no entry — carry-over, there is one — a conflict hint. Values from `properties.json` are never carried over: the old version writes them into the header as well. The cost is about 140 bytes per note in the group.
- **З37. The remaining risk.** The old version shows the value from its own `properties.json` and on any property edit, even an icon, rewrites the header with that value (`upsert` writes `value`). If the value in the header was meanwhile changed in the new version, the old one will silently return; the previous one will remain only in that device's history. Only writing the values to `properties.json` could close this, and that changes the owner's decision З22. The owner's decision (З.10-2): З22 stays; the warning on the sync screen (З22) and the migration report (З19) add the phrase "На устройствах со старой версией Amby не меняйте свойства — она может вернуть прежние значения" (On devices with an old version of Amby, do not change properties — it may bring back the previous values).

**Migration (З19–З21)**

- **З38. Before the migration and with sync.** Until the owner confirms the migration, properties work the old way (`properties.json`, the current code), there are no groups. With sync enabled, the migration is offered only after a successful pass; if `layout.json` has already arrived from the server (another device migrated), there is no migration of its own — the unsynced local edits to `properties.json` are carried over by З36. The migration holds the sync lock (Д9) the whole time.
- **З39. The same result on two devices.** Groups and marks are built only from `properties.json` and the headers, property `id`s are preserved; two devices that migrated the same vault without a connection write identical group bytes and `layout.json` (except `hint`, which produces no conflict, З31).
- **З40. "Matches" — by YAML meaning.** `property_value_to_yaml_value(value, propertyType)` equals the parsed value of the key; an empty string, `null` and an empty value match. Otherwise lists, flags and numbers would end up en masse in "differs". The value is not written to the header if, per З25–З26, it cannot be changed without loss — the note goes as corrupted (З20). Editing many headers may raise the Д54 question on the next pass — the З19 report warns about this in advance; the protection is not weakened.
- **З41. Log and retry.** The log stores the plan of header edits: path, hash before, hash after. Retry: the hash equals "after" — done; "before" — do it; otherwise (the note was edited between the failure and the retry) — do not touch, put it in the summary. Backups and the log are in `.amby/migrations/<id>/` (local). An unreadable `properties.json` or an unknown version — the migration does not start, nothing is written. No `properties.json` — the migration is empty, `layout.json` is written before the first group.
- **З42. Rollback.** Before `layout.json` — on retry from the log: only groups whose bytes equal those written by the migration are deleted; headers are restored from the copies only if their hash equals "after". After completion — a manual action in the review: the groups go to the vault trash (not deleted), a header is restored only if it was not changed after the migration, the rest — as a "not restored" list. With sync these are ordinary deletions under the Д54 protection.

**Scale**

- **З43. 1,000,000 notes.** A third with presentation, about 200 bytes of presentation and 140 bytes of the З36 mark: a group about 0.45 MB, all groups about 115 MB, the same amount of sync bases on the device. An edit is a write, encryption and upload of one group. The panel reads from SQLite (З23); a group is parsed only on write. A full walk of the groups is only an index rebuild, the migration and the З10 review. The З30 limits are not reached here. The numbers are confirmed by the З.8 benchmark, the gate threshold is assigned after the first measurement.

**Dismissing with "No" in the З3а hint (the open item is closed)**

- **З44. The dismissal is stored in the note's entry, shared by all devices** (`dismissedRenames`, as recorded in З3а). Why not only on this computer: SQLite is rebuilt — the hint would come back after a rebuild; a local file is not synced — the hint would come back on every device; and the dismissal is a decision about this note's data. Refinements: merged as a set without conflict (З31); a pair is removed only on a real write of this note (not a background one, З8), when one of the names is absent from both the entries and the header keys; the hint is shown only for a non-empty value and exactly one pair "dormant entry — new key". The cost is one group write per click.

### З.10. Questions for the owner after the review

1. ~~**Mass deletion of properties (З32).**~~ — **decided 04.11: option (а)**, a question as in Д54. How to show the stop: (а) like the current question about mass changes: "Another device is deleting properties from N notes. Continue?" — the recommendation; (б) a conflict card per note (with hundreds of notes — hundreds of cards).
2. ~~**An old version may bring back an old value (З37).**~~ — **decided 04.11: option (а)**, З22 unchanged, the phrase in the warning and the report. (а) Leave З22 as is and add to the warning on the sync screen and to the migration report the phrase "На устройствах со старой версией Amby не меняйте свойства — она может вернуть прежние значения" (On devices with an old version of Amby, do not change properties — it may bring back the previous values) — the recommendation; (б) while the account has an old version, the new one also writes the values to `properties.json` (changes З22, more code and writes).

### З.11. Format revision: template, differences only, 16 JSON Lines files (4 November 2026)

The owner's decisions the same day, after the review: (1) a **base-properties template** in the app; an entry exists only for a note whose presentation departs from the template, and stores only the differences; editing a property's settings changes only that note; (2) **16 groups instead of 256**; (3) **JSON Lines**, files `prop_<0–f>.jsonl`. The owner's other decisions do not change: values only in the header, each note has its own presentation, no per-note file, no encoding in the key name, no shared configurable per-name schema. They replace З5–З8; they refine З2, З4, З9, З16, З30, З31, З36, З43. The technical details are review decisions (delegated by the owner).

**Template**

- **З45. The base-properties template.** The list of base properties (for example "Статус" (Status), "Приоритет" (Priority), "Дата" (Date), "Теги" (Tags)) with type, icon and options lives **in the app code**, in one place in Rust (`src-tauri/src/property_template.rs` or nearby), and is given to the interface via IPC; it is not written to the vault. A base property has names in all interface languages (compared per З24: NFC, then exact, then ASCII case-insensitive) and options per language; the options are taken in the language of the matched name. A header key with no match in the template is text without an icon (as now). The template **may be changed** in new versions (owner's decision): notes without differences will get a different icon or options. Values in the header are never rewritten because of a template change; a value outside the options is shown as is with the mark "нет среди вариантов" (not among the options). Different versions on two devices may show different presentation — the data does not change.
- **З46. How a property is assembled in the panel.** For each header key: the value — from the header (З1); type, icon, options — from the template (or text without an icon); on top — the differences from the note's entry (З47). A property from the entry that is not in the header is dormant (З3). A template property without differences whose key was deleted simply disappears: there is nothing to lose.

**Format**

- **З47. A note's entry is one line.** `{"id":"<amby-id>","hint":{"path":"…"},"props":{"<name>":{…differences…}},"order":[…]}`. `props` is a dictionary "property name → differences": only the fields that depart from З46 (`propertyType`, `icon`, `settings`), plus the service field `previousName` (З9) and unknown fields (preserved). There is no property `id` (ULID) anymore: a property is recognized by its name, like a header key; names in `props` are unique after NFC, ASCII case-insensitively. A difference equal to the template is not written; a property without differences is removed from `props`. `order` — only if the user rearranged the properties themselves: a list of names; names from the header that are not in `order` come after, in header order; names from `order` that are in neither the header nor `props` are skipped on reading and removed on a real write. `hint` — as in З7 (only the path), not a key. `dismissedRenames` — as in З3а. There is no line if there is none of `props`, `order`, `dismissedRenames` (З4).
- **З48. Files.** `.amby/properties/prop_<x>.jsonl`, `<x>` is the first hexadecimal digit (lowercase) of the BLAKE3 of the `amby-id` string (UTF-8): 16 files, a file exists only if it has entries. `.amby/properties/layout.json`: `{"version":1,"groups":16,"hash":"blake3-first-hex","template":1}` (one line), read before the groups; a different number of groups, an unknown version — properties are read-only (as in З6). The first line of each group file is `{"version":1}` (plus unknown fields, preserved); then entry lines, **sorted by `id`** bytewise.
- **З49. Canonical form of a line.** Compact JSON without spaces, object keys in ascending order (bytewise by UTF-8), one line feed at the end. Two entries equal in meaning give identical bytes. The file's BOM and line ending are preserved (as in З8); a new line is written with the file's ending.
- **З50. Writing by lines.** An edit of one note replaces, inserts or deletes **only its line**; the other lines are copied byte for byte, without re-parsing or rebuilding. The write is atomic for the whole file (`frontmatter::atomic_write_bytes`), there is no background normalization (З8).
- **З51. A corrupted line.** A line that does not parse, repeats the `id` of another line, breaks the order or violates З47 (non-unique names) — the app shows the other entries of the file, the note of this line has read-only properties, **the whole file is not written** (property edits of the notes of this group are rejected with a warning), and the vault review (З10) shows the line and its number. Nothing is repaired automatically.

**Sync**

- **З52. Merge (refines З16, З30, З31).** Only the paths exactly `.amby/properties/prop_<0–f>.jsonl` are merged. The З30 tolerance — for the whole file (any corrupted line per З51 in any of the three versions — a conflict set, Ж28), line parsing, the unit is a note's line (by `id`), comparison by JSON meaning; the З16 and З31 rules (`hint`, `dismissedRenames`) unchanged. The result: lines by `id`; a line taken from one side unchanged is carried over with its bytes; one composed anew — in the canonical form З49. Short paths (as in Ж11) return the side's bytes. A line (and the file's first line) equal in meaning on both sides is taken like this: the bytes of one side equal the bytes of the base — the bytes of the other; the bytes of the sides are equal — those; otherwise — the canonical form З49. This makes the result symmetric (Ж10) and independent of whose computer is merging. The canonical form is mandatory only for lines composed anew or chosen by the last rule; a line carried over with a side's bytes may be non-canonical (a person or another program edited it). The size limit is `PROPS_MERGE_MAX_BYTES` per file. Protection against mass loss — per pass (З32), counting `props` elements (properties with differences).
- **З53. Old-version marks — in separate files (refines З36).** The `legacy` marks are stored not in the presentation lines but in `.amby/properties/legacy_<x>.jsonl` (the same group digit as `prop_<x>.jsonl`): the first line `{"version":1}`, then `{"id":"<amby-id>","entry":"<hex>","record":"<hex>"}` by `id`. `record` is the BLAKE3 of the canonical line of the note's entry (З49) without `hint` and `dismissedRenames`, or 32 zeros if there is no entry. This way a note that has nothing to store in presentation (everything as in the template) has no empty tombstone line in the group; the `legacy_*` files are merged the same way (З52), and when no old versions remain, they can be removed in one action (a separate decision later). The З36 rules are unchanged.

**Migration and scale**

- **З54. Migration (refines З20).** An entry from `properties.json` is converted into differences from the template (З47) in the language of the matched name; a property that fully matches the template gives no line; the order from `properties.json` is written to `order` only if it differs from the header order. Property `id`s are not carried over (they no longer exist); the `id`s of the old entries stay in `properties.json` and in the backup. The З39 determinism is preserved: the template is part of the code, so `layout.json` stores the template number (`template`), and two devices with different template numbers do not migrate the same vault without a connection: a migration with a template number lower than in the arrived `layout.json` does not start.
- **З55. Rename and re-binding without a property `id` (refines З9, З35).** Rename: in `props` the key changes to the new name with `previousName`; if there are no differences and the property is not a base one — the header key is changed, and a line is written to the group only for the duration of the operation (with `previousName`) and then removed. Re-binding (З35) moves the whole line from the group of the old ID to the group of the new one (it may be the same group).
- **З56. Scale (replaces З43).** A line is 100–150 bytes. 1,000,000 notes, differences in 5%: a group about 0.3 MB, all groups about 5 MB; the extreme case of 30% — a group about 2 MB. An edit is a write, encryption and upload of one group. The numbers are confirmed by a benchmark.

**What changes in the tests (З.8).** Format: the path `prop_<x>.jsonl` and 16 groups; `layout.json` with `template`; the first line; sorting and the canonical form of a line; writing by lines — other lines are byte-for-byte unchanged; a corrupted line (З51). Template: a property without an entry takes type, icon and options from the template; names in different languages; a difference equal to the template is not written; a template change does not touch the header; a value outside the options. Writing: only differences, `order` only after rearranging, no property `id`. Merge — by lines (З52), `legacy_<x>.jsonl` (З53). Migration — into differences (З54), different template numbers. The tests on property `id`s and the `<g>.json` format from commit `4d48633` are rewritten for З45–З56 in a separate commit with an explanation (section 6, step 6).

### З.12. The migration runs by itself, without intermediaries (5 November 2026, owner's decision)

After a manual check in the built app (errors at the joint of the old panel and the new format). It replaces the confirmation in З21 and "before the migration — the old way" in З38; the rest of З19–З22, З36, З38–З42, З54 remains in force.

- **З57. The migration starts by itself** the first time a new version opens the vault, without confirmation: the З19 pre-check (no writes) → backups, journal, writing per З21 → `layout.json` last. The З38 conditions still hold: with sync enabled, only after a successful pass; if a `layout.json` arrived from the server, there is no migration of its own. The migration runs in the background, and properties are read-only while it runs.
- **З58. What the person sees.** After the migration, a single notice above the bottom bar (К3): «Свойства переведены в новый формат» (Properties converted to the new format). If values in the old file and in the header differed, the notice says «Расходились значения у N заметок, оставлены значения из шапки» (Values differed in N notes, header values kept) and has a report button; in the report, each such note has the action «Взять значение из старого файла» (Take the value from the old file) (an ordinary value edit, З1). The choice before the migration (З20, "for each one you can pick the value from the file") no longer exists: the value from the file is not lost — it stays in `properties.json` and in the backup and is available from the report. The З42 rollback is unchanged — manual, from the vault check.
- **З59. Cannot be converted** (`properties.json` is unreadable or of an unknown version, waiting for a sync pass): properties are shown **read-only** from `properties.json` by the old reader, no edits, and a notice with the reason. The new version still never writes `properties.json`.
- **З60. No shims.** The old property commands (`upsert_custom_property`, `delete_custom_property`, `reorder_custom_properties` on top of groups, `property_store` for writing) are not used: the properties panel calls the new-format commands by property name (create, rename, change type, change presentation, write value, order, delete, "Restore", "Delete permanently"). The compatibility layer (`property_groups::upsert_compat` and related) is removed. `property_store` remains only for reading the old file (З59, migration, З36).

- **З61. Icons by type (5 November 2026, owner's decision).** Template icons and default icons come from the app's Lucide set (`amby-icon:<name>:`, with no color of their own — the property's text color), not emoji. A property without an icon of its own gets the icon of its type, the same set as in databases (`property-type-icon.tsx`, one place for notes and databases): text `align-justify`, number `hash`, checkbox `square-check`, date `calendar-days`, link `link-2`, select `circle-chevron-down`, list `list`. The template's base properties take the icon of their type, except "Priority" (`flag`). The person's own icon wins (З46). Refines З45 ("text without an icon" → type icon). Relation and status for note properties are separate work, not here.
- **З62. Select options and multi-select (5 November 2026, owner's decision).** (a) "Select" has no empty option "—", neither in the list nor as the default. (b) Options are set through fields: one field is one option, with an "Add option" button below; a property of type "Select" or "Multi-select" is not created or saved without at least one non-empty option (a clear error at the field). (c) One option can be marked as the standard one (a small icon to the right of the field, toggles on and off); when a property is created, the standard option is written to the header (for multi-select, a list containing it); without a standard option, the value is empty. (d) New type "Multi-select": the value in the header is a YAML list (`[a, b]` or `- a` lines, as `property_value_to_yaml_value` writes for `list`), type icon `list` (З61); the existing type `list` is shown as "Multi-select" with options, and old values are not rewritten. (e) Option storage: `settings` stays a string (З47); the new form is JSON `{"options":["…"],"default":"…"}` (options may contain commas); the old "comma-separated" form is read as before and is rewritten to the new form only on a real edit of that property's options; the template (З45) stores options in the new form. Values in the header are not rewritten because of the change of `settings` form.
- **З63. Option color and the value picker (5 November 2026, owner's decision).** (a) The user chooses the color of an option's tag; until chosen, the color is derived from the option text in a stable way (the same text gives the same color on all devices). (b) A color is a name from the theme's short palette (`gray`, `brown`, `orange`, `yellow`, `green`, `blue`, `purple`, `pink`, `red`), not a color code: the shades themselves for the light and dark themes live in `src/themes/`. (c) Storage: the JSON `settings` (З62 e) gets an optional field `"colors":{"<option>":"<name>"}`; it holds only options with a chosen color; an unknown color name or a color for a nonexistent option is read as "not chosen" and kept unchanged until the options are edited. Renaming an option carries its color over, deleting it removes the color. The template (З45) stores `colors` the same way; difference from the template works as for any `settings` field (З46). Merge is unchanged: `settings` is one field of the record (З52). (d) The value picker for "Select" and "Multi-select": at the top, the selected values as colored tags with a cross (removes the selection); below them a search field with the hint «Выберите вариант или создайте новый» (Choose an option or create a new one); below that the list of options as tags with a ⋮⋮ handle for ordering; a click adds the option to the value (for "Select" it replaces), typing new text and pressing Enter creates the option and selects it; order and new options are written to `settings`, the value to the header. Values in the header that are not among the options are shown as a gray tag and are not lost.

### З.13. Errors found in manual testing on two computers (6 November 2026)

The owner tested Mac and Windows: renaming is "slow", the icon does not arrive, values arrive every other time, an error when editing a stale property. Two causes are given below; tests were written first (`src-tauri/src/sync/vault_fs_tests.rs`, `sync/sim/tests/props_groups.rs`, vitest).

- **З64. Groups did not reach the server.** The vault walk in the app (`VaultFs`, the `AMBY_SYNCED_FOLDERS` list) did not enter `.amby/properties/`, although the core rules (`is_excluded_path`, З14) allowed that folder: `state.db` held only the folder itself, and `prop_a.jsonl` lived separately on the two computers without any conflict. Fixed: `properties` was added to the list. So that the mismatch cannot recur, a test checks every `.amby/<x>/` folder against the core rules: "the walk enters" ⇔ "the core does not exclude". `property-ops/` and `migrations/` are still not synced.
  - **First meeting of already-converted vaults.** Both devices have groups, there is no base, and the rows of the same note differ. Verified by a test (`sync/sim/tests/props_groups.rs`, "a_vault_already_converted…"): the general rule of two files at one path (Д43) applies, not a per-note merge. The server's version stays at the path, the **local file as a whole** goes into a conflict set of kind `bothCreated`; nothing is lost, there is no false "mass loss" stop (З32), and the second computer has the same file after the pass. Price: without a base, no per-note merge is done, so edits to different notes of one group also land in one set, and the choice "from this / from the other" gives away one side as a whole (the other side's edits stay in the set until it is closed). Groups get a base after the first shared pass; from then on everything is as in З16–З17. Replaced by rule З64а below.
- **З65. The panel rereads properties after others' changes.** (a) Reloading a clean open note after an external change (`use-vault-data.ts`) also rereads that note's properties. (b) Watching `.amby/` stays closed. Instead, `VaultFs` remembers which `.amby/` files a batch of incoming changes wrote and, before the end of the batch, hands them to the app (`FsHooks::amby_written`); `AppHooks` emits the event `sync-amby-written` with a list of paths relative to the vault (like `sync-merged`, without specta). The UI (`SyncNoticesHost`), on paths `.amby/properties/…` or `.amby/properties.json`, rereads the properties of all open notes (`reread-note-properties.ts`). This does not overwrite a local edit in progress: a note with an unfinished edit is skipped, and a response that arrives after an edit started or the vault changed is discarded (`propertyVersion` and the edit queue). The note text and its revision are not touched. The event arrives once per batch; a repeated reread is harmless.
- **З66. Edit by a stale name.** If a command answers "No such property" (the property was renamed or deleted on another computer), the panel is reread and says: «Это свойство изменили на другом компьютере. Панель обновлена, повторите правку.» (This property was changed on another computer. The panel has been updated, repeat the edit.) The card «Не удалось сохранить свойство» (Could not save the property) is not raised in this case. Other failures are as before.
- **З67. The sync notices host was not connected (6 November 2026).** A check with temporary diagnostics: the pass writes `.amby/properties/prop_a.jsonl` and emits `sync-amby-written`, but the panel does not reread properties. Cause: since 23 October `SyncNoticesHost` lived only in `workspace-shell.tsx`, which is not rendered anywhere; tests rendered the host directly. Because of that, none of its events worked in the app: rereading properties (З65), the notice about a new conflict, the warning about an open note deleted on another computer, returned notes, limits. Fixed: the host is rendered in `workspace.tsx` next to the orchestration (in both layouts) and removed from `workspace-shell.tsx` so that there are not two. The test `src/components/workspace/workspace.test.tsx` checks that the entry point mounts it.
- **З64а. First meeting of groups — a merge against an empty base (6 November 2026, owner's decision "close the loose ends").** When a device has its own group file at a path (`prop_<x>.jsonl` or `legacy_<x>.jsonl`) that the server has not yet seen, and the server already has a different one at that path (Д43), the engine does not put the local file into a set as a whole but merges both versions per note (З16, З52) against the empty base `{"version":1}\n` (`EMPTY_GROUP`): rows of different notes and identical rows of the same note merge without a set, different rows of the same note are a conflict of that note. On a conflict, everything is as before per Д43: the server's version is at the path, the local file as a whole is in a `bothCreated` set, but the set now stores the empty base so that notes can be chosen one by one (З17). An unreadable side is as in З33. Tests: `sync/sim/tests/props_groups.rs` ("a_vault_already_converted…", "the_marks_of_a_vault_already_converted…").
- **З68. Measuring the migration on a large vault (6 November 2026).** `src-tauri/src/property_groups/tests_scale.rs`, `migration_of_ten_thousand_notes` and `migration_of_fifty_thousand_notes` (`#[ignore]`, run manually in release). Three properties per note, and in every tenth note the header lacks the key of one property (the migration writes to the note). Result (Mac, release): **before the fix** preview 10,000 — 8.4 s, 50,000 — 113 s, opening with migration 67 s and 351 s; growth was quadratic: the check for a duplicate `amby-id` (`ensure_unique_identity`) for every note scanned the whole `notes` table. Fixed: lookup by primary-key range (`id >= prefix AND id < prefix;`), same answer. **After:** preview 0.9 s and 3.8 s, migration 56 s and 208 s, growth linear. What remains is writing each changed note with all the safeguards (a "before" copy to the journal, a history snapshot, an atomic write with flushing the file and folder to disk), ≈40–55 ms per note; in the measurement every tenth note changes. If every note in a vault changes, 10,000 notes take on the order of 7–9 minutes. The safeguards are not weakened; **question for the owner:** whether to show migration progress and whether to keep the window from closing while it runs.
- **З50 closed.** An edit to one note changes only its row, and the others are copied byte for byte (`files.rs`, `write_line`); the file is written atomically as a whole — that is by design (З50), there is no partial file write and none is needed.
- **З69. Per-note choice in the app (7 November 2026, owner's decision).** Until now, a property-group conflict set in the app was resolved only with the whole file («Оставить с этого компьютера» (Keep from this computer) / «Взять с другого» (Take from the other)), and the unchosen side's edits to notes that did not conflict were lost. Now, if a group's set (`prop_<x>.jsonl`, `legacy_<x>.jsonl`) has a base (`bothEdited` or a first meeting per З64а), the app resolves it per З17 through the core's `resolve_group`:
  - **Port** (`FileConflictStore`): `read_group(id)` returns the list of conflicting notes `{noteId, notePath|null, noteTitle|null}` (path from the row's `hint.path` at the path, otherwise from the second version; title is the file name without `.md`) and how many rows were merged automatically. A set without a base, not a group, or an unreadable version is `not_group`: the two whole-file buttons remain. The resolution `Resolution::Notes { picks }`: `picks` maps `noteId → current | other` for **every** conflicting note; an incomplete choice is `incomplete_picks`, nothing is written. The result is `resolve_group` (the rest from the clean merge, canonical form), written as with other resolutions: history snapshot, the set's files to the trash.
  - **Session:** `read_group_conflict(id)` returns the same list and a `stamp`; `resolve_group_conflict(id, picks, stamp)` writes only if both files have not changed since they were shown (`conflict_changed`, as with comparison).
  - **UI:** for such a conflict, sync settings show, instead of two buttons, a list of notes, each with «С этого компьютера» (From this computer) / «С другого» (From the other), and a «Готово» (Done) button, available once all are chosen. Below the list, how many changes were merged automatically. The answer `conflict_changed` makes the list reread.

- **З70. The last edit wins (7 November 2026, owner's decision).** A check on two computers showed that the picker window is unnecessary, and the sets are synced, so the labels "this / other computer" cannot be determined in it. The owner decided: if a note's presentation was changed on two computers, the **last** edit stays, with no window and no notices.
  - **Edit time:** a note's row in `prop_<x>.jsonl` gets a new optional field `edited` — Unix time in milliseconds of the row's last edit by the clock of the computer that made the edit. The app sets it in `update_entry` every time the row changes (a write with no change does not touch it); the migration and rows from old versions do not have it. The field is not part of the row's meaning: rows that differ only in `edited` merge without a conflict; in the `record` of the `legacy_<x>.jsonl` marks (З53) it is not counted, like `hint` and `dismissedRenames`. A non-numeric `edited` is treated as absent.
  - **Merge** (`merge_group`, only `prop_<x>.jsonl`): a note changed differently on both sides is no longer a conflict. The row with the greater `edited` wins; on a tie, the row whose text is greater bytewise (the merge stays symmetric, the result is the same on both computers). A row without a time loses to a row with a time. If one side removed a row and the other side's changed row has a time, the changed one stays (an edit preserves data). If neither side has a time (both edits were made by an old version), the former conflict per З17 remains. The `legacy_<x>.jsonl` marks are still resolved by a set.
  - **Nothing is lost:** the losing row stays in the history of the group file (a snapshot before overwriting by the merge, as in З16).
  - **Old sets** created before З70 remain and are resolved by the З69 window (their rows have no `edited`).
  - **Clocks:** with a wrong clock, the edit with the later time wins even if it was made earlier. For property presentation the owner considers this acceptable.

## Appendix И. Previous versions and deleted files from the server (Э5)

Owner's decision of 9 November 2026: versions from the server are shown **in note history**, deleted
files from the server are restored **from the archive**. Written before code (section 6, strictly): this entry → review →
separate red-test session → commit of tests with owner approval → separate code session with
frozen tests. The server gets nothing new: everything needed is already there (П33, П35, П36).

### И.0. Why and what already exists

- The server stores previous versions of each record and deleted records for `history_days` days (П35, П36, П57-г), but
  the app does not show them: on this computer there are only local history snapshots and the trash. A new
  computer, an emptied trash, or history turned off — and there is nothing to restore the previous text from, even though it sits on
  the server (promise О4).
- The engine keeps tombstones of deleted records forever (Д3) and already properly accepts a revived record (Д37).
  Therefore **restoring is an ordinary local file write**, which a pass sends like any edit.
  There is no new path in the engine, no new request on the server, and no new format.

### И.1. Note versions from the server

- **И1. Where.** In the history panel of the open note — a single chronological list: local snapshots and versions from
  the server. A server version is marked with a cloud icon and a device label («этот компьютер» (this computer) / «другое
  устройство» (another device); device name — when the server knows it). The time is the server's `committed_ms` (for display only,
  Р3).
- **И2. Which versions.** A `versions` request (П33) by `item_id` of this note's record from engine state. Into
  the list go versions that (a) are not `deleted`, (b) have a body, (c) have a header that passed core verification (Ф14,
  Ф19, Д65; otherwise the version is silently skipped and logged with a code), (d) have any header path
  (a renamed note shows its history under previous paths, label «называлась …» (previously named …)).
- **И3. No duplicates.** A version whose `content_hash` equals the current file contents on
  disk is not shown, nor is a version whose `content_hash` equals the contents of a local snapshot of this note (then the
  snapshot is shown, as it is closer and requires no network). The snapshot's hash is computed on display, not stored separately.
- **И3а. Cloud on matching snapshots (9 November 2026, owner's decision after testing on two computers).**
  On a computer with history, almost every server version matches a snapshot (`sync-overwrite`,
  autosave), and under И3 the cloud was not visible at all. Now a matching version still does not get
  a separate row, but **a snapshot whose hash equals a version on the server gets a cloud** with the label «есть на
  сервере» (on server) and the device of that version (И1). If the current file equals the latest version on the server, at the top
  of the list there is one quiet line «Текущий текст сохранён на сервере» (Current text is saved on the server). Offline there are no clouds; И4 applies.
- **И4. When offline or sync is off.** Local snapshots are shown as usual; instead of
  server versions — one quiet line at the end of the list («Версии с сервера появятся, когда синхронизация
  включена» (Server versions will appear when sync is enabled) / «…когда будет связь» (…when connected)), without a notice (`docs/notices.md`: nothing for the user to do). The request
  is made when opening the panel and is not retried on its own; a network error shows the same line.
- **И5. Preview.** Selecting a server version downloads its body, decrypts it in memory, and verifies BLAKE3
  against the header's `content_hash` (as in Д14). On mismatch, the version is marked «повреждена» (corrupted); it cannot be previewed or
  restored, while everything else works. Preview is the same as for a snapshot (`snapshot-preview`), read-only.
  Bodies beyond the memory limit (Д71) are not downloaded: «слишком большой файл для просмотра» (file is too large to preview).
- **И6. Restore.** Same as restoring a local snapshot (`restore_snapshot`): (a) the current file
  contents are first saved as a snapshot with reason `restore` (even if history is off); (b) the version's bytes
  are written atomically to the **current** note path byte-for-byte; (c) the watcher and index are updated as when
  restoring a snapshot. If the file on disk changed since the list was shown (a different `content_hash` than when
  read), restore is not performed: the list is re-read, and the user sees «Заметка изменилась, список обновлён» (Note changed, list updated)
  below the button. A note open in the editor with unsaved edits is saved first (as currently done
  before restoring a snapshot). When sync is off, restore returns «недоступно: синхронизация выключена» (unavailable: sync is disabled) (`SyncOff`); when offline, `Offline`; no file is written and no snapshot is taken.
- **И7. Then — normal sync.** The restored text is an ordinary local edit: a pass sends
  it as a new version based on the current one (Р3). If someone else's edit arrived in the meantime — the usual rules Р7 and
  К1 (merge or conflict), nothing special.
- **И8. Notes only.** In the first version — for files that have a history panel (`.md` notes).
  Attachments, canvases, and other files — later (the "Later" section of the map).

### И.2. Deleted files from the server in the archive

- **И9. Where.** In the archive panel — a separate section «На сервере» (On server) below the local trash. Shown
  only when vault sync is enabled; otherwise there is no section.
- **И10. Which files.** File tombstones (not folders) from engine state (Д3) whose latest version according to `versions` (П33)
  is `deleted`, while among earlier ones there is a version with a body. The **last version with a body**
  before deletion is taken: path, size, deletion time (`committed_ms` of the deletion version), device that deleted it (author of the deletion version). A record
  that is no longer on the server (`404`, removed by П36 cleanup) is not shown.
- **И11. No duplicates with trash.** A file is not shown if the local trash (`.amby/trash/`) has
  an entry with the same original path and the same contents (BLAKE3 of the body equals the hash of the file in trash): it is
  restored from trash as before. A file that currently sits at its own path with the same
  contents (already restored) is also not shown.
- **И11а. Cloud in trash (9 November 2026, owner's decision).** A local trash entry that matches a deleted file on the server
  under И11 gets a cloud «есть на сервере до …» (on server until …) (retention per И12); it still has no separate
  row in the «На сервере» (On server) section. Offline or with sync off, there are no clouds.
- **И9а. Toggle instead of a section (10 November 2026, owner's decision after testing).** The «На
  сервере» (On server) section below the trash no longer exists. In the archive panel header — a button with a cloud (icon, tooltip «Файлы на
  сервере» (Files on server)), visible only when vault sync is enabled. Clicking toggles the panel between
  the local trash and the list of deleted files on the server (И10–И17); the button stays pressed while the server list
  is shown; clicking again returns to the trash. Panel search searches the currently shown list. The choice is not
  remembered between launches. If sync is turned off while the server list is shown, the panel
  switches back to the trash on its own.
- **И10а. User files only (10 November 2026, fix after testing).** Paths inside `.amby/`
  (conflict sets, property group files, layouts, and other internal files) are **never** shown: neither in
  the server list (И10), nor with clouds (И3а, И11а), nor as local trash rows in the archive panel (trash entries
  remain on disk and are removed by normal trash cleanup). Restore (И15) does not accept an internal path,
  even if passed one: response «нельзя вернуть» (cannot restore), nothing is written.
- **И10б. One path — one row.** If a file at the same path was deleted several times (each restore is
  a new record, И16), the server list shows one row — for the most recent deletion (highest
  `seq` of the deletion version).
- **И12. Retention.** Each row shows «удалён N дней назад, хранится до …» (deleted N days ago, kept until …) (deadline = deletion time +
  `history_days` from `account`, П61; if no `account` — `retention.version_days` from `info`, П9). This is a hint:
  server cleanup decides.
- **И13. Loading the list.** One `versions` request per tombstone, at most 8 concurrently, when opening
  the section; the result is cached in memory until the next sync pass. More than 500 tombstones —
  the 500 most recent by deletion `seq` are shown, plus the line «показаны последние 500» (showing the last 500). Offline — same line
  as in И4.
- **И13а. The list does not go stale (10 November 2026, fix after testing on two computers).** An open
  archive panel re-reads both the local trash and the server list after each sync pass
  (sync state event; a batch of events is debounced over 300 ms), and re-reads the server list on every
  cloud button click as well. This adds no server requests: the app answers from its cache until
  tombstones change (И13). Previously an open panel showed a list minutes out of date, and a file that
  sync moved to trash appeared only after reopening the panel.
- **И14. Preview.** Same as И5 (notes — as text, other files — file metadata without contents).
- **И15. Restore.** The body is downloaded, decrypted, verified (И5); then the file is written atomically
  **without replacing** (no-replace) at the original path, creating missing folders. If the path is occupied — the file is placed next to it using the import rule (`free_path` in `commands/import_files.rs`): `Имя 2.md` (Name 2.md), `Имя 3.md` … in the same folder, leaving the existing file untouched (restoring from trash does not pick a new name, it refuses). The user sees the final name below the button. With sync off, restore returns «недоступно: синхронизация выключена» (unavailable: sync is disabled) (`SyncOff`) for each file and writes nothing. Watcher and index — as in `restore_trash`. Note properties (styling in groups,
  З) are not restored by this action: they live in their group file and are restored via its history.
- **И16. Then — normal sync.** The engine sends the file at the tombstone path as a revived record
  (`push`: `deleted = false` based on the deletion version) or as a new one — both outcomes are valid; other
  devices receive the file through the normal path (Д37 treats it there as "returned"). The only requirement: no
  device deletes it again.
- **И17. Several at once.** Rows can be checked and restored together; each — separately per И15, an error on one
  does not cancel the others; the summary is a single line below the button («Возвращено 5 из 6, одна повреждена» (Restored 5 of 6, one corrupted)). The mass-change pause
  (Р8) does not block restore: this is creating files, not deleting them.

### И.3. Where the code is

- **И18. Core (`sync/core`).** The server port gets `versions(item) -> Vec<VersionEntry>` (fields П33).
  The pure function `server_history(entries, keys) -> Vec<HistoryVersion>` opens and verifies headers
  (И2) and lays them out; the pure function `deleted_files(tombstones, histories)` builds the И10 list.
  Opening a body with hash verification uses the existing core body-opening function. Neither function writes to disk.
- **И19. App (`src-tauri/src/sync/`).** Commands (Specta, final names for tests):
  `sync_note_versions(path) -> NoteServerVersions { available: bool, reason: Option<…>, versions }`,
  `sync_read_server_version(path, item_id, version) -> ServerVersionText`,
  `sync_restore_server_version(path, item_id, version, expected_hash)`,
  `sync_deleted_files() -> DeletedOnServer { available, reason, files, truncated }`,
  `sync_restore_deleted(item_ids) -> RestoreDeletedOutcome`. Writing to disk goes through existing
  atomic history and trash functions (`restore` snapshot, no-replace), under the same `mutation_gate`.
- **И20. UI.** `src/components/workspace/history/` (list with cloud, preview, «Вернуть» (Restore)) and
  the archive panel («На сервере» (On server) section). Texts are in `resources.ts` (ru, en), notices per
  `docs/notices.md`: the response to «Вернуть» (Restore) is below the button; there is no raw error text.

### И.4. Tests before code (what must be red)

| Where       | What is tested                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `sync/core` | И2: deleted versions, versions without a body, and ones with a forged header (wrong key, version mismatched with AAD) are excluded; a renamed record yields all paths; И3: hash duplicates are removed; И10: a tombstone with a body before deletion yields a row, without a body — no, `404` — no; И5: body with wrong hash — «повреждена» (corrupted), not text |
| `sync/sim`  | И7: restoring a previous version on A after someone else's edit on B — merge or conflict per Р7, nothing is lost; И16: restoring a deleted file on A — the file appears on B and is no longer deleted on any device, including after full reconciliation (Д58)                                                                                                    |
| `src-tauri` | И6: `restore` snapshot before write, version bytes on disk byte-for-byte, refusal when file changed; И15: no-replace, occupied path — free name, folders created; И11: file from trash and already-restored file are not in the list; И4/И13: offline `available = false`, without error; И17: partial success is counted accurately                              |
| UI (vitest) | cloud and device label on a server version; «нет сети» (offline) line instead of versions; «На сервере» (On server) section in archive only when sync is enabled; «Вернуть» (Restore) shows summary below the button                                                                                                                                              |

### И.5. Questions for the owner

Resolved on November 9: the place for versions (note history) and the place for deleted items (archive). The technical decisions И3, И6,
И11, И15, И16 were made by delegation (data safety matters more than convenience) and are put to the owner
only if the review challenges them.

## Appendix К. Archive separate from deletion

The owner's decision of November 10, 2026, after the Э5 check on two computers. Currently «В архив» (Archive) and
«Удалить» (Delete) (in the «Корзина хранилища» (Vault trash) mode) put the file in one place — `.amby/trash/`, and sync also
puts other devices' deletions there (Р8), and the app puts closed conflict sets there. So the archive mixed archived,
deleted and service items. Recorded before code (section 6, strict: data transfer and migration): this entry →
review → red tests → commit of the tests with the owner's approval → code with the tests frozen.

### К.1. What is what

- **К1. The archive is what a person put away but wants to keep.** The «В архив» (Archive) action moves a file (or folder) to
  `.amby/archive/<former path>` — the path inside the archive repeats the path in the vault, so «Вернуть» (Restore) puts the
  file back in its former place. The archive is **synced** like ordinary files (Р4 is extended with the folder
  `.amby/archive/`): moving to the archive is a rename, and on the other computer the file also ends up in the
  archive. There is no time limit: the archive is not cleaned up automatically.
- **К2. Deletion is what a person removed completely.** «Удалить» (Delete) does what is chosen in the «Удаление файлов» (File deletion)
  setting (К4). Deleted items are **not shown** in the archive. While sync is on, they can be restored
  through the cloud in the archive panel (appendix И) within the server's retention period.
- **К3. Another device's deletion** (the file was deleted on another computer) puts the local copy into the **system trash**
  of this computer (the macOS Trash, the Windows Recycle Bin, freedesktop `trash` on Linux) — this **changes Р8** (previously
  always `.amby/trash/`). If the system trash is unavailable (there is none on this system, a network drive, the
  system refuses), the copy goes to the service `.amby/trash/` — **never erased permanently** (the Р8 protection remains:
  another device's deletion does not destroy data). The rule "a deletion is applied only if the file was not modified"
  (Р8, Р7) does not change.
- **К4. The «Удаление файлов» (File deletion) setting** — three options: «В системную корзину» (To the system trash) (default),
  «Удалять полностью» (Delete permanently) (as now, with confirmation every time), «Переносить в архив» (Move to archive) (then «Удалить» does the same
  as «В архив»; the buttons are duplicated — the person's choice). The former value `vault` («Корзина хранилища» (Vault trash))
  is read as «Переносить в архив». The setting is not synced (as now, each computer has its own).
- **К5. The service `.amby/trash/`** remains only for service items: closed conflict sets (Г), copies
  when the system trash refuses (К3) and former entries that cannot be moved (К7). It is not
  shown in the archive panel. Its cleanup is as now.

### К.2. The "Archive" panel

- **К6.** The panel shows **only** `.amby/archive/`: files with their folders, the date of moving to the archive
  (the time of the marker file, К8), search. «Вернуть» (Restore) moves the file to its former path without replacing anything: if the path is taken, next to it
  under a free name (the `free_path` rule, as И15); missing folders are created. «Удалить из архива» (Delete from archive) —
  per the К4 setting (the «Переносить в архив» option here means "to the system trash"). The cloud (И9а) remains:
  it shows files deleted on the server. The cloud badge on archive rows (И11а) is gone — the archive is already
  synced; for files deleted on the server, И11 checks against the archive: a file that lies in the archive with the same
  content is not shown in the server list.
- **К8. Move time.** For each archive file, a record `.amby/archive/.meta/<id>.json`
  `{path, archivedAtMs}` is stored (synced together with the archive); if there is no record (the file came from outside), the file's time is used.
  Unknown fields of the record are preserved.

### К.3. Transition (migration, once per vault)

- **К7. What is moved.** Each `.amby/trash/` entry whose original path is **outside** `.amby/` is moved to the
  archive: `.amby/archive/<original path>` (if that is taken — a free name next to it), with a К8 record, where
  `archivedAtMs` = the former deletion time. Service entries (original path inside `.amby/`) stay in
  `.amby/trash/`. An entry without content or with a corrupted description stays in place and is written to the log
  with a code.
- **К9. Transition safety** (migration rules, AGENTS.md): a transition version in `.amby/migrations/`;
  a preliminary check (how many entries, how much space); the move is by renames inside the vault,
  without copying, one entry at a time, with a step log so that an interrupted transition continues from the same place;
  a completion marker; the outcome is recorded (moved, left, why). Rollback: reverse renames per the
  log. No entry is deleted.
- **К10. Sync and the transition.** The transition runs before the first sync pass after the update.
  Moved files go to the server as new archive files. On the second computer the transition does the same with its
  trash; identical files at one archive path are linked by Д15 (the same path and hash —
  nothing is written), different ones — an ordinary conflict (Р7), nothing is lost.

### К.4. Questions and what changes in the previous

- Changes: Р8 (К3), И9/И11/И11а (К6), the deletion setting (К4). Does not change: appendix И (versions and
  items deleted on the server), the conflict rules, history.
- Resolved by the owner on November 10: a hidden folder `.amby/archive/` (rather than a visible "Archive"); another device's deletion — to the
  system trash; the former trash is moved to the archive; three deletion options.
- **К11. Clarifications after the red tests (November 10, 2026, by delegation).** (a) The `path` of the К8 record is the path
  of the file itself in the archive (with «Имя 2.md» ("Name 2.md") if the place was taken). (b) Another device's deletion of a **service** path
  (`.amby/…`) goes to `.amby/trash/`, not to the person's system trash (К3 applies only to the person's files). (c) If the
  system trash refuses on «Удалить из архива», the file stays in the archive, with a «Не удалось удалить» (Could not delete) button below it.
  (d) After «Вернуть» and «Удалить из архива», the file's `.meta` record is removed. (e) The former trash descriptions
  after the move lie in `.amby/migrations/<version>/` together with the log — they are needed for rollback. (f) Rollback is a function and
  a command for the developer; there is no button in the interface. (g) The transition runs when the vault is opened, before
  sync and the watcher start, on every vault (including one that is already synced). (h) The former
  tests that describe the old archive model (`archive-panel*.test.tsx`, `archive-preview.test.tsx`, the
  `recycle_bin::list_for_archive` tests) are rewritten by the implementation for К — this is not a violation of the freeze; the new
  К tests stay frozen. (i) In the app tests, `VaultFs` gets a refusing system
  trash, in production — the real one.

### К.5. After the check on two computers (November 10, 2026, owner's decisions)

- **К12. No «Файл был удалён вне Amby» (File was deleted outside Amby) window.** For an open note whose file disappeared (deleted on another
  computer or outside Amby), there is no window any more — only a card in the note's notices (docs/notices.md,
  the «Нужно решение» (Needs a decision) level, expands itself once). The card has two buttons: «Вернуть» (Restore) (saves the
  open text at the former path, like the former «Восстановить локальную» (Restore local)) and «Закрыть заметку» (Close note). Text by
  reason: deleted on another computer — «Эту заметку удалили на другом компьютере. Текст ещё открыт здесь»
  (This note was deleted on another computer. The text is still open here) (not "in the vault archive": another device's deletion now goes to the system trash, К3); deleted outside
  Amby — «Файл удалён вне Amby. Текст ещё открыт здесь» (The file was deleted outside Amby. The text is still open here). While there is no decision, autosave is paused,
  as now; edits are not lost.
- **К13. An open note was moved to the archive on another computer (November 10, 2026, owner's decision).** A move
  to the archive arrives as a file relocation to `.amby/archive/…` (К1), not as a deletion, so the tab must
  neither follow the file into the archive nor close silently. The tab stays with the open text; in the note's
  notices there is a card «Заметку перенесли в архив на другом компьютере» (The note was moved to the archive on another computer) (the «Нужно решение» level, expands
  itself once, a copy in the bell) with buttons «Вернуть из архива» (Restore from archive) (the file returns from the archive to its
  former path by the К6 rule, without replacing anything) and «Закрыть заметку». While there is no decision, autosave writes
  neither to the archive nor to the former path; edits are not lost. A move to the archive **on this** computer closes
  the tab, as now.
- **К14. The tab finds a relocated note later too (November 11, 2026, after the check).** If an open
  note's file disappeared and it is marked "deleted", and then (at any next tree refresh) a note with
  the same `amby-id` appeared at another path — the tab switches to it and the deletion card is removed.
  Reason: on Windows a relocation (super note, Д70а) reaches the watcher as two events, and the Д70а check
  runs before the new file appears.
- **К15. Deleting a folder does not close notes open in it.** When sync deletes a folder (including
  a whole super note), an open note from it behaves as on the deletion of a single note (К12):
  the tab stays with the text, the card «Эту заметку удалили на другом компьютере», a copy in the bell.
  Silently closing a tab because of another device's deletion is never allowed.
- **И10в. A super note is one row.** In the list of files deleted on the server (the cloud), the files of one super note
  (the folder `Имя/` with `Имя.md` and its canvas, sketch, database views and attachments) are shown as **one**
  row by the note's name; «Вернуть» restores all its files together (each by И15, without replacing anything).
- **К3а. A super note to the system trash — as one folder.** If all files of a super note's
  folder are deleted in one pass, the whole folder goes to the system trash, not each file separately; a refusal of the
  system trash is as in К3 (to `.amby/trash/`, nothing is erased). If only some of the
  files are deleted — one by one, as now.
- **И9б. The «Архив / Облако» (Archive / Cloud) switch and a shared counter.** Instead of the single cloud button (И9а), the archive header has
  a switch of two icons, like the «заметка / база» (note / database) switch (archive and cloud), visible
  only when sync is on; without it — the header as before. The counter in the header shows the
  number of rows of the **shown** list: the archive or the items deleted on the server.
- **Д70а. A super note is a relocation, not a deletion.** Turning a note into a super note (`ensure_bundle`:
  `Имя.md` moves into a folder) writes the Д70 rename hints the same way as a rename and
  a move, before the move on disk. The other computer receives a relocation of the same record, not a deletion and a new
  file. Protection on the receiving side: if an open note's file disappeared but a note with the same `amby-id`
  exists at another path (relocated), the tab switches to it without a deletion notice.

## Appendix Л. Deleting a space from the server from the app (Э5)

The owner's decision, November 12, 2026: add deleting a server space from the app;
**local files are not deleted**. Supplements П53 and У4. The section 6 order is strict: contract → a separate
tests session and a red run → review and a separate commit of the tests → another session for implementation.
The current package prepares the contract, stub interfaces and tests, without implementing deletion.

### Л.1. Server

- **Л1. Request.** `DELETE /v1/spaces/{space_id}` with the owner's device token, no body.
  Success — `200 {"ok":true}`. The server does not receive the space's plain-text name or a local path.
- **Л2. Access.** No token or an invalid one — `401 unauthorized` (a revoked device —
  per П5). Someone else's, unknown, already deleted space and an invalid ID — equally `404 not_found`
  per П6. Authentication precedes the ID check. No changes on refusal.
- **Л3. Full deletion.** Only the chosen space is deleted, along with its current records, versions,
  saved commit responses, ready bodies, incomplete uploads and parts. After success the space
  is absent from `GET /v1/spaces` and `GET /v1/account`; old requests to it respond `404 not_found`.
  The used space and the space count are freed immediately. The data and keys of other spaces and accounts
  are preserved; the account and devices stay connected.
- **Л4. Atomicity and races.** Deletion is serialized with commit and upload by the existing vault
  lock: records and request bodies completed before the deletion are deleted together with the space;
  write requests to it after the deletion get `404 not_found`. After a restart it does not appear
  again. File cleanup
  uses the existing mechanism for deleting server bodies; a failure is not passed off as success.
  An open SSE stream of the deleted space is closed; the streams of other spaces keep working.
- **Л5. Repeat.** A repeated DELETE — `404 not_found`, with no side effects. A lost response can safely be
  repeated: the app client may treat this response as confirmation that the chosen
  space is absent. Other errors are not turned into success. An old server (`404`/`405` without deleting) is not
  considered to have performed the action: the app rechecks that the ID is absent from the list of spaces.

### Л.2. The app and the safety of local data

- **Л6. Before the request.** If the space of the open vault is being deleted, the app stops its
  session, waits for the running pass to finish and saves the disabled state before the DELETE.
  After a restart, sync of this vault does not resume by itself. If the disabling cannot be
  saved reliably, the delete request is not sent. Deleting another space does not disable
  sync of the open vault.
- **Л7. Local files stay.** Neither success, nor refusal, nor a lost response deletes, moves
  or rewrites Markdown, attachments, the archive, history, properties, conflicts and recovery
  of the local vault. Open tabs, unsaved text and note autosave remain.
  Only changes to the local sync link metadata are allowed, not a cleanup of `.amby/`.
- **Л8. After the request.** On a confirmed deletion, the open vault's link with this space
  is forgotten, and the sync state is updated by an event. The next enabling is an explicit choice of
  a new or an existing space. On error the link is kept disabled so that the action
  can be repeated; there is no automatic re-creation of the space or upload of files.
  Other local vaults and devices see the space's absence on the next pass,
  confirm the ID's absence in the list of spaces, save `enabled=0`, stop exchanging with it
  and keep all local files; after a restart the exchange does not resume by itself. Deleting a space is not
  a deletion of every note in the change feed.
- **Л9. Boundaries.** Client: `SyncClient::delete_space(token, space_id) -> Result<(), ClientError>`.
  App: `SyncRuntime::delete_space(space_id: &str) -> Result<(), SyncError>` (async),
  then the Specta command `sync_delete_space` and `SyncPort.deleteSpace(spaceId)`. The command uses
  the current account; the ID is strictly validated before any state change and before the request. A concurrent change of account or vault, or a repeated
  click, must not apply the result to a new session. Keys and tokens are not passed to the interface.

### Л.3. Interface

- **Л10. Place and confirmation.** The row menu in «Хранилища аккаунта» (Account vaults) → «Удалить с сервера…» (Delete from server…).
  The shared confirmation window (`confirm`, `confirmations.ts`, the rules of `docs/notices.md`) names the
  vault, shows the size of the current data and history and requires typing its exact name
  (У4). If the name is not decrypted, the ID is shown and confirmation is by typing the ID. The text states explicitly:
  «Копия на сервере и её версии будут удалены навсегда. Локальные файлы на устройствах останутся» (The copy on the server and its versions will be deleted permanently. Local files on devices will remain).
  The buttons are «Удалить с сервера» (Delete from server) and «Отмена» (Cancel), initial focus on cancel; without a matching input, deletion is
  unavailable. Closing the window does nothing. Do not create a separate confirmation host.
- **Л11. Result.** While the request is in progress, repeating is unavailable. After success the list and quota are re-read;
  a result line below the list: «Копия на сервере удалена. Локальные файлы остались» (The copy on the server was deleted. Local files remain).
  An error — by code under the action, without raw server text and without a duplicate toast. For an unknown
  outcome, it is reported that the server deletion is not confirmed, while local files are kept and the exchange
  of this vault is paused. Switching the account resets the old confirmation and result.

### Л.4. Checks

- Server/conformance: Л1–Л5 — access, isolation, deletion of current/old/incomplete bodies, quota,
  repeat, restart, races, and closing the SSE of only the chosen space.
- Client: Л1, Л2, Л5, Л9 — DELETE of the right ID and authorization, errors keep their code.
- App: Л6–Л9 — a real server and temporary vaults, byte-for-byte preservation of user
  files, stopping the current session before deletion, another active session is not stopped, a repeat and
  an old server do not give false success, re-activation does not create a space.
- Interface (a separate package after the server tests): Л10–Л11 — confirmation by name, cancel,
  one request, quota update, the error in one place, a stale response after switching the account.
- Natively on two computers: delete the server copy while a modified note is open;
  on both devices the files and text remain, after a restart the exchange does not resume by itself.

## Appendix М. First sign-in without a temporary device name (Э2б)

The owner's decision, November 12, 2026 (the "simplify the first sign-in" item from Э2б). Currently a new
device signs in twice: under a temporary name, to obtain and open the AK, then under the real
name, and it disconnects the temporary device. The name is encrypted with the AK, and the AK arrives only in the sign-in response.
The section 6 order is strict: rules → a separate tests session and a red run → review and commit of the
tests with the owner's approval → code.

### М.1. Server

- **М1. The name is optional.** In `POST /v1/auth/login` (П14) and `POST /v1/auth/recover` (П15) the `device` object
  and the `device.name_enc` field are optional. Without them the device is created with an empty name
  (`name_enc` of zero length); `"device": null` means the same as absence. The response and everything else — as before. A present `name_enc`
  is validated as before (Ф34): an invalid form — `400 bad_request`. `enroll` (П12) does not change: there the name
  is still required.
- **М2. Identical refusals are preserved.** The presence or absence of a name does not change the response to invalid
  credentials (П14, П15), attempt limits (П16) and the fake salt (П13).
- **М3. A device without a name.** In `GET /v1/devices` (П19) its `name_enc` is an empty string; the name is set by the
  existing `PUT /v1/devices/{device_id}` (renaming one's own device). An empty name cannot be set via
  `PUT` (as now).

### М.2. Client

- **М4. Sign-in.** `sign_in` makes **exactly one** `login` without a name, opens the AK, then sets the name
  via `PUT /v1/devices/{own id}`. If the AK did not open — the device is disconnected, the error is
  `cannot_decrypt` (as now). If setting the name failed — the device is disconnected, the sign-in is not
  considered completed, the error is returned with its code. In no outcome does the server keep
  more than one device from this sign-in.
