- **М5. Recovery.** `recover_account` makes exactly one `recover` without a name, opens the AK,
  changes the password (`PUT /v1/keys`, П18), then sets the name. A failure of any step after `recover` disconnects
  this device.
- **М6. Old server.** A server that requires a name answers a sign-in without it with `400 bad_request`.
  The same for `recover`. Then the client repeats the former path (temporary name → AK → sign-in under the real name → disconnecting
  the temporary one). Other responses (`401`, `429`, network) are not retried and do not change the path.
- **М7. Boundaries.** Client: `SyncClient::login_unnamed(username, auth_key)` and
  `SyncClient::recover_unnamed(username, recovery_auth_key)` — requests without `device`. The signatures
  of `sign_in` and `recover_account` and the app do not change.

### М.3. Checks

- Conformance: sign-in and recovery without a name → `200`, the device in the list with an empty name, then a
  rename; an invalid present name — `400`; invalid credentials without a name — the same `401` as
  with a name; `enroll` without a name is still `400`.
- Client: by the request log — one `login`/`recover` without `device`, then one `PUT` of the name and no
  `DELETE` on success; a refusal at the name step and an unopened AK disconnect the device; an old server
  (`400` on sign-in without a name) goes through the former path; `401` without a name does not turn into a retry.

## Appendix Н. Subscription: free account, sync by plan (November 13, 2026, owner's decision)

The owner returned to monetization (deferred on November 5) in a narrow form: registration stays open, but
only an account with a subscription can **sync**. There is no payment yet: the subscription is granted and removed by the
server administrator, the buy button in the app is a stub. Strictness — as for the server (Э2) for
Н1–Н9 and the engine; the app (Н10–Н12) — lightweight. Decisions П50–П61 apply, except for what is directly
changed here. Along the way most of finding Б1 of the security review is closed: an account without a
subscription does not take up disk space on the server.

### Н.1. Server

- **Н1. Plan flag.** A plan (П50) gets an optional field `sync` (`true`/`false`). No field —
  `true`: existing plan files and a server without a file (a virtual plan with no limits, П52) work
  exactly as before. A non-boolean value is a file error (П51, startup refusal).
- **Н2. Pin expiry.** In `users` a new column `plan_until_ms` (integer, may be empty).
  Empty — the pin is indefinite. Set and `now_ms ≥ plan_until_ms` — the pin has **expired**: the default
  plan applies (as if `plan` were empty), while the columns are not rewritten (expiry does not
  depend on cleanup and does not lose the record of who was granted what). Without a pinned `plan` the expiry has no effect.
- **Н3. Commands.** `user plan set <name> <plan> [--until YYYY-MM-DD]`: pins; with `--until` access
  is in effect **through that day inclusive** — until 00:00 UTC of the next day; without `--until` the expiry is removed.
  A date in the past or of an invalid form — code 2, nothing changes. `user plan set <name> --default` removes
  both the plan and the expiry. `user plan show` and `user list` (and `--json`) show the expiry (`plan_until_ms`) and
  the expiry flag (`plan_expired`) — in `--json` as fields of the user record next to `plan`; in the plain
  output of `user list` — as the date of the last day. In the README, a subscription means a pin to a plan with `sync: true`.
- **Н4. What is closed without sync.** If the effective plan has `sync: false`, the refusal
  `403 subscription_required` goes **only to adding** requests: `POST /v1/spaces`,
  `POST …/blobs`, `PUT …/parts/{n}`, `POST …/complete` and `POST …/commit` that contains at least one
  non-deletion record. **A commit of deletions only** (every record has `deleted: true` and no `blob_id`)
  goes through even without a subscription (the owner's decision of November 13: so that a person can export their files and
  remove everything from the server). Х6 is clarified: without a subscription, only existing live records can be deleted. A mixed commit is rejected entirely, nothing is applied. Everything else
  works as usual: sign-in, recovery, keys and password change, devices, login,
  `GET /v1/account`, `GET /v1/spaces`, the feed, events, versions, downloading bodies, `DELETE` of a body and
  `DELETE` of a space. Nothing is deleted or shrunk by itself (as П57): data on the server
  stays until the subscription is renewed or until the user deletes it.
- **Н5. Order of checks.** Token (`401`) → space ownership (`404`, П6) → subscription (`403`) →
  the route's previous checks. The subscription check and the write are performed **in one transaction** (as П27):
  a subscription removal applied before the transaction rejects it; a repeat of a commit with the same `commit_id`
  accepted earlier (П28) answers with the same response even without a subscription. The `403` body is an ordinary П4 error,
  without details about the plan.
- **Н6. `GET /v1/account` (П61).** `plan` gains `"sync": bool` (the effective plan) and
  `"until_ms": N | null` (the pin expiry; `null` — no expiry or no pin). After expiry — the
  default plan, `pinned: false`, `until_ms: null`. `GET /v1/info` does not change.
- **Н7. Migration `SCHEMA_V3`** (`user_version` 2 → 3) under the П58 rules: a `VACUUM INTO` copy with
  `quick_check` before the migration, one transaction, a `last_migration` record. Only
  `ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN plan_until_ms INTEGER;` — existing rows are not rewritten.
  Rollback: the previous image will not start on version 3 (`TooNew`), so rollback is restoring the `pre-v3-*` copy.
  The row counts of all tables before and after the migration match.

### Н.2. Client and engine

- **Н8. Client.** The code `subscription_required` in the protocol's `error_code`; `AccountView.plan` gets
  `sync: bool` and `until_ms: Option<i64>`. An old server without these fields — `sync: true`, `until_ms: None`
  (Р13): old servers are not turned into "no subscription".
- **Н9. Engine.** `AccountInfo` gets `sync_allowed: bool`. If `false` (from `account()` before
  sending or from a `403 subscription_required` response at any send step), the pass **does not send
  new and modified items** — neither bodies nor commits containing them — but **does send deletions** in a separate commit
  of deletions only (Н4; like Д75 for a full quota), and receiving proceeds as usual. After a `403` at one
  step the pass does not try the remaining new and modified files. Each file waiting to be sent gets
  the reason `UnsyncedReason::SubscriptionRequired`; incomplete uploads are not deleted from the device,
  a `403` response is not considered a pass failure and does not turn on growing retries (Д19). The state on the
  device loses nothing: after the subscription is granted, the next pass sends what has accumulated.

### Н.3. App (lightweight)

- **Н10. Account.** In the «Аккаунт» (Account) zone, a plan row: without a subscription — «Без подписки · синхронизация недоступна»
  (No subscription · sync unavailable) and the button «Оформить подписку» (Subscribe); with a subscription — the plan name and «до 13 декабря 2026» (until December 13, 2026) when there is
  an expiry. The price is not written in the app: it is set by the server's owner, and a self-hosted server may not
  sell at all. Login, device names, password, sign-out and the vault list are always available.
- **Н11. Purchase stub.** «Оформить подписку» opens one calm line below the button: «Оплата скоро появится. Пока подписку выдаёт администратор сервера»
  (Payment is coming soon. For now the subscription is granted by the server administrator). No windows, navigation or network requests.
- **Н12. Vault.** Without a subscription, enabling sync for a new vault is unavailable: in place of
  the switch, the same line and button. For an already enabled vault the status is «Нужна подписка — изменения ждут отправки»
  (Subscription needed — changes are waiting to be sent) at the «Внимание» (Attention) level (per the [notice rules](notices.md): one place, no repeated
  announcements); receiving and restoring versions from the server work. «Проверить снова» (Check again) re-reads the account.
  All strings — in `src/locales/resources.ts` with all translations.

### Н.4. Checks

- Conformance: a plan with `sync: false` → the five adding requests get `403 subscription_required`, a commit
  consisting only of deletions goes through, a mixed one is rejected as a whole, all
  other Н4 routes as usual; `sync: true` and a server without the file — unchanged; someone else's
  space without a subscription — `404`, not `403`; a repeat of an already accepted commit without a subscription — the same response as before;
  `GET /v1/account` with `sync` and `until_ms`; a pin with an expired term acts as the default plan,
  with an unexpired one — as the pinned plan.
- Commands: `--until` (inclusive, UTC), a past or invalid date — exit code 2 with no changes, `--default`
  removes the term, the output of `user list`/`user plan show` and `--json`; a plans file with a non-boolean `sync` — rejected.
- Migration 2 → 3: a copy before the migration, all previous rows and columns preserved, `TooNew` for the previous code.
- Client: the fields, and an old server without them; the error code.
- Engine: without a subscription new and changed files are not sent, deletions are sent, received changes are applied, every waiting file has a reason,
  no retries with a pause; granting a subscription — the next pass sends everything accumulated without loss.
- App: behavior tests by element identifiers (the button, the stub row, the vault status).

## Appendix Т. Fixes from the security review: Б2 and Б3 (13 November 2026, owner's decision)

The findings are in the [Э5 security review](road/target/sync-security-review.md). The strict order of section 6:
server, client and engine. Decisions П, Д and Л remain in force, except where explicitly refined here.

### Т.1. Б3: ownership and write in one transaction

- **Т1. Re-check inside the write transaction.** Every request that writes to a space
  (`POST …/blobs`, `PUT …/parts/{n}`, `POST …/complete`, `POST …/commit`, `DELETE …/blobs/{id}`,
  `DELETE /v1/spaces/{id}`), inside the same transaction in which it writes, looks the space up again by
  the pair `(space_id, user_id)` and checks that the device token is still valid. No space — `404
not_found`, token revoked — the same `401` as before; nothing is written. The row number found before reading the request
  body is not used for the write. This refines Л4: a write request started before the space was deleted and
  finished after gets `404`, not `500`, and never writes to a different space — even if another user's new
  space received the same internal database row.
- **Т2. Body parts.** A part file written to disk for a space that has been deleted in the meantime
  does not make the response successful (`404`) and is removed by the usual leftover cleanup (as is done now for failures).
- **Т3. No migration.** The database schema does not change; outward behavior for ordinary requests stays the same.

### Т.2. Б2: a large commit must be readable by other devices

- **Т4. Outgoing commit size (engine).** Outgoing changes are split into commits of no more than
  `min(max_commit_items, 1000)` records **and** no more than 1 MiB of request JSON (by an estimate of the record size:
  the header hex and service fields). The order of Д18/Д50 is kept: when a folder is renamed, the folder
  record goes first, then the files by path; the recipient survives any order (Д50). This refines Д18: "the folder
  rename batch goes in one commit if there are no more than `max_commit_items` records" is read with this
  limit.
- **Т5. Feed response (client).** The client accepts a `GET …/changes` response of up to 96 MiB (a commit
  within the server's default `max_json_bytes`, 64 MiB, plus the feed's service fields); all other responses stay
  at the previous 4 MiB. More than 96 MiB — the same "response too large" error as before, with no crash and no reading into
  memory beyond the limit. This is also how spaces where a large commit already sits on the server are read.

### Т.3. Checks

- Conformance (Т1, Т2): a commit started with a slowly transmitted body, during which the space is
  deleted and another user creates a new one, — `404`, the new space has no records at all and its
  `seq` is unchanged; the same for `POST …/blobs` (the new space has no body row, the second
  user's quota is untouched) and `PUT …/parts/{n}` (`404`); a write request after the space is deleted — `404`,
  not `500`; a token revoked during body transfer — `401`, nothing written.
- Engine (Т4): the first connection of a vault with 6,000 files sends commits of no more than 1,000 records
  and no more than 1 MiB; the second device receives all files byte for byte; long paths reduce the number
  of records in a commit; a rename of a folder with 2,500 files arrives whole; if the folder has its own record (Д45:
  a folder that used to be empty), it goes first.
- Client (Т5): a 10 MiB feed response is read; more than 96 MiB — `bad_response` with no crash; other responses
  over 4 MiB — still an error.

## Appendix Х. Server limits: Б1, Б4, Б5 (13 November 2026, owner's decision)

The findings are in the [Э5 security review](road/target/sync-security-review.md). The strict order of section 6.
Decisions П, Е, Н and Т remain in force, except where explicitly refined here. The new settings are read from the
environment (П40); each has a default value at which an ordinary own server works as before.

### Х.1. Б1: disk space and the number of accounts

- **Х1. Free-space reserve.** `AMBY_SYNC_MIN_FREE_BYTES` (default 1,073,741,824 — 1 GiB; `0`
  turns it off). If the volume of the data directory has less free space than this, **adding** requests get
  `507 server_storage_full`: open registration, `POST /v1/spaces`, `POST …/blobs`,
  `PUT …/parts/{n}`, `POST …/complete` and a commit containing a non-deletion record. Sign-in, reading,
  downloading and deletions (a commit consisting only of deletions, `DELETE` of a body and of a space) keep working so that space
  can be freed. The check happens before the write, in the same transaction as the route's earlier checks;
  the response is an ordinary П4 error with no details about the disk. Free space is measured on Unix (Linux, macOS); on other
  systems the check treats the space as sufficient (the server runs in Docker on Linux).
- **Х2. Number of accounts under open registration.** `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_USERS` (not set by default — no
  limit). When the number of users (`users` rows) is already this many or more, open registration without a code
  answers `409 registration_unavailable` (as Рег2) and creates nothing. Administrator invitations and
  registration by code are not limited.

### Х.2. Б4: size and number of simultaneous request bodies

- **Х3. Sign-in requests.** The bodies of `POST /v1/auth/prelogin`, `…/enroll`, `…/login`, `…/recover` — no more than
  64 KiB, regardless of `max_json_bytes`; larger — `413 payload_too_large`, nothing is created and the
  attempt does not count toward the sign-in limit.
- **Х4. Simultaneous large bodies.** A JSON body larger than 1 MiB (by `Content-Length` or as it is read)
  is read only if no more than `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_LARGE_BODIES` such bodies are being read at the same time (default
  4); otherwise immediately `503 server_busy` with `Retry-After: 5`, and the body is not read into memory. Bodies up to 1 MiB,
  body parts (`PUT …/parts`, which are no larger than `PART_SIZE` anyway) and event streams are not limited by this
  counter. The client meets `503` as before (`server_unavailable`, retry on the next pass).

### Х.3. Б5: headers in the quota and deletions without a subscription

- **Х5. Cancelled (owner's decision, 13 November).** Header lengths are **not** included in space accounting: the П55 accounting
  and the history cleanup by `max_history_bytes` (П57-д) stay as before. Counting headers in history
  diverged from the cleanup (which freed only bodies) and could erase extra earlier versions. The remainder of Б5 — database
  bloat from headers (folders, renames) only on an account with a subscription, deliberately; it is
  limited by Х6, the history term and the administrator's removal of the subscription. Separately, on the owner's server the subscription
  plan has a history limit (`max_history_bytes`), because history (earlier bodies) is not part of the quota.
- **Х6. Deletion without a subscription — only of what exists.** A commit consisting only of deletions without a subscription (Н4)
  is accepted if each of its records deletes an **existing live** record: `base_version` equals
  the current version, it is at least 1 and the current version is not a deletion. Otherwise — `403 subscription_required`
  as a whole, nothing is applied (nothing to delete means this is not freeing space but adding).
  With a subscription, the previous rules П26–П30 are unchanged.

### Х.4. Checks

- Х1: with a threshold above the free space, each of the six adding requests — `507
server_storage_full`, while sign-in, the feed, download, a commit consisting only of deletions, `DELETE` of a body and of a
  space — work as usual; `0` turns it off; by default the ordinary tests pass as before.
- Х2: with a limit of 2 and two users, open registration — `409 registration_unavailable`, by invitation code
  — goes through; without a limit — as before.
- Х3: a sign-in body larger than 64 KiB — `413` with no failure recorded; ordinary sign-in works.
- Х4: while `AMBY_SYNC_MAX_LARGE_BODIES` slow bodies larger than 1 MiB are open, the next large one — `503
server_busy` with `Retry-After`, while small requests go through; after they close — it goes through again.
- Х6: without a subscription, deletion of an existing live record — `200`; deletion of a new one (`base_version = 0`),
  an already deleted one, or one with a wrong version — `403 subscription_required`, nothing applied.

## Appendix Ц. Password for dangerous actions: Б10 (13 November 2026, owner's decision)

Finding Б10 of the [security review](road/target/sync-security-review.md): deleting the server copy of a
space required only the device token, and a password change did not offer to disconnect the other
devices. The owner decided: deletion from the server requires the current password; after a password change the app
asks whether to disconnect the other devices. Server and client — the strict order of section 6, the app —
lightweight. Л1–Л11 remain in force, except where refined here.

### Ц.1. Server and client

- **Ц1. Password proof on deletion.** `DELETE /v1/spaces/{space_id}` accepts a JSON body
  `{"auth_key": "<64 hex>"}` — the sign-in key of the current password (like `PUT /v1/account/username`). The order: token
  (`401`) → the space is the user's own (`404`, П6) → body (`400 bad_request` if it is missing or the field is of the wrong kind) →
  attempt limit by account name and address (`429`, П16) → the key is compared in constant time with the
  hash of the sign-in key; a wrong one — `403 invalid_proof`, the attempt counts toward П16, nothing is deleted →
  deletion per Л3–Л4 and Т1. Success clears the pair's counter (like sign-in).
- **Ц2. Client.** `SyncClient::delete_space(token, space_id, auth_key)` sends the Ц1 body.
  `flows::password_proof(client, token, password) -> AuthKey` derives the sign-in key from the password by the salt and
  the account parameters (`GET /v1/keys`) and checks the password **on the device** by opening the AK wrapper, like the username
  change: a wrong password — a `403 invalid_proof` error with no request to the server. The password is not held longer than
  the call and does not end up in the log or in errors. The app obtains the key before stopping the exchange (Л6), so
  a typo in the password does not turn synchronization off.
- **Ц3. Old server.** A server without Ц1 ignores the body and deletes as before; the new client is not
  broken by this. An old client without the body gets `400` on a new server.

### Ц.2. App (lightweight)

- **Ц4. Deletion from the server.** The dialog «Удалить с сервера…» (Delete from server…) (Л10) asks for the current
  password in addition to typing the exact name. The button is enabled when both are entered. A wrong password — the line «Неверный пароль» (Wrong password) below the field, the dialog
  stays open, nothing is deleted; `429` — a line with the wait time. The command
  `sync_delete_space(space_id, password)`; the password is not stored. The stopping of the exchange before the request (Л6)
  is undone on an `invalid_proof` or `429` refusal: the vault's synchronization returns to its previous state
  (on, if it was on), and the link to the space does not change.
- **Ц5. Disconnect the other devices after a password change.** If, after a successful password change, the account
  has other devices, the app asks: «Отключить остальные устройства?» (Disconnect the other devices?) with their number, with buttons
  «Отключить» (Disconnect) and «Оставить» (Keep). «Отключить» revokes the tokens of all devices except this one (`DELETE
/v1/devices/{id}`, П19); the disconnected ones stop synchronizing and ask to sign in again, their files
  remain. If some failed to disconnect — a line with the count and a «Повторить» (Retry) button. With no other devices
  there is no question.

### Ц.3. Checks

- Conformance: deletion without a body and with a body of the wrong kind — `400`, the space is intact; a wrong key —
  `403 invalid_proof` and a counted attempt (after `login_max_failures` wrong ones — `429`, even with the correct
  key); the correct one — `200`, the space is deleted; someone else's space with the correct key — `404`; the order
  `401` → `404` → `400` → `429` → `403`.
- Client: the request body contains the sign-in key derived from the password; a wrong password — an error with the code
  `invalid_proof`; the password is not in the error text.
- App: the delete button is unavailable without a password; a wrong password — a line, the dialog is open, no deletion,
  the open vault's synchronization is turned back on; after a password change with two other devices — the question, «Отключить» revokes both and does not touch the
  current one; with no other devices — no question.

## Appendix Ч. Low-risk server findings: Б6, Б7, Б8 (22 November 2026, owner's decision)

The findings are in the [Э5 security review](road/target/sync-security-review.md). The owner asked to fix
all three. The outside behaviour changes only where stated (Ч2, Ч3); the protocol stays version 1.

- **Ч1. Б6: database work does not stall the runtime.** Every database call and write transaction, the
  wait for the connection lock included, runs through `block_in_place` when the server runs on the
  multi-threaded runtime: the worker hands its other tasks (other requests, event-stream pings) to another
  thread while it blocks. Commands and tests without a runtime run the work directly. Test: a 400 ms
  database call on a runtime with one worker does not stop a timer task.
- **Ч2. Б7: at most four event streams per device.** A fifth stream of the same device ends the oldest
  one at once; the other four keep receiving events. Another device of the same account has its own four.
  The limit is fixed (`STREAMS_PER_DEVICE = 4`), not a setting. A client that opens one stream per open
  vault is never affected.
- **Ч3. Б7: a stalled request body is refused.** `AMBY_SYNC_BODY_TIMEOUT_SECS` (default 60, a positive
  whole number): when a request body sends nothing new for that long, the request ends with
  `408 request_timeout` and nothing of it is applied. It covers JSON bodies and body parts. The README of
  one's own server keeps the advice to set proxy limits as well.
- **Ч4. Б8: temporary files of their own.** A part and a joined body are written to a temporary file with
  a unique name (`.part-N.<nonce>.tmp`, `.body.<nonce>.tmp`) that is removed when the write fails, so two
  uploads of the same part or two `complete` calls never write into one file. `complete` already refuses a
  body whose row was deleted in between (`404`, the number of updated rows is checked).

Tests (written first and seen failing on the old code): `sync/server/src/db.rs` (Ч1) and
`sync/conformance/tests/server_limits_ch.rs` (Ч2–Ч4). Deploying to Omlet is a separate step with the
owner's permission.
